			(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang WHERE CountryId = C.cmpCountryId AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') Country,  
			(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang WHERE CountryId = C.cmpCountryId AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') cmpCountry,  
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang WHERE ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') Province, 
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang WHERE ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') cmpProvince, 
			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') City,
			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') cmpCity, 
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang WHERE UserId = C.cmpReferUserId AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') ReferUser,
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM Users_Lang u where u.UserId = C.UserId AND u.LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') cmpUser,
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM Users_Lang u where u.UserId = C.cmpFirstRegisterUserId AND u.LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') cmpFirstRegisterUser,
			C.CompanyPersonID
			,C.CompanyPersonId prsId
			,C.CompanyPersonID
			,C.prsFName
			,C.prsLName
			,ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = C.prsTitleId), '''') +
				'' '' + ISNULL(C.prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ ISNULL(C.prsFName, '''') PrsName
			,C.prsTitleId
			,C.prsJobId
			,C.CompanyPersonId prsId
			,C.prsTel
			,C.prsTelDesc
			,C.prsTelII
			,C.prsMobile
			,C.prsFax
			,C.prsNationalCode 
			,C.prsPreTel
			,C.prsTelUpTo'
	SET @clmn = @clmn + '
			,(
				SELECT TOP 1
					ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId), '''') + '' ''+
					ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName, '''') 
				FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
				WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID)
			) PersonTop1Order,

				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
				END) cmpName,

				(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
				END) cmpNameWithTitle, 
			C.cmpNumber,
			(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''') 
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
			END) cmpTitle, 
			C.cmpFame,
			C.cmpPreTel, 
			C.cmpTel, 
			C.cmpTelUpTo,
			C.cmpPreTelII, 
			C.cmpTelII, 
			C.cmpTelUpToII,
			C.cmpMobile, 
			C.cmpFax,	 
			(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryId = C.cmpCountryId) Country,  
			(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId) Province, 
			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId) City, 	
			C.cmpAddress, 
			C.cmpReferUserId,	
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''')
			FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId) ReferUser, 
			C.prsLName,
			C.prsFName,
			C.prsTel, 
			C.prsTelDesc, 
			C.prsTelII, 
			C.prsMobile, 
			C.prsFax, 
			RTrim(C.PrjTitle + '' '' + IsNull(prjPostFix, '''')) PrjTitle,
			C.CompanyPersonId prsId,
			C.ProjectId prjId
			,C.cmpNationalCode 
			,C.[cmpUserName]
			,C.[cmpCode] 
			,C.cmpTelDesc
			,C.cmpTelIIDesc
			,(SELECT ISNULL(PJ.Jobs, '''') FROM CrmPersonJobs_Lang PJ WHERE PJ.PersonJobID = C.prsJobId AND PJ.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') prsJob
			,C.prsPreTel

						(SELECT RTrim(PrjTitle + '' '' + IsNull(prjPostFix, '''')) FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang WHERE ProjectID = Prj.ProjectID AND LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') PrjTitle, 
						0 prsId,
						prj.ProjectId  prjId,
						0 ConversationID 
						,null prsNationalCode 
						,C.cmpNationalCode
						,C.[cmpUserName]
						,C.[cmpCode] ' 
			Set @clmn = @clmn + '
				,(SELECT cmpTelDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CCLN.LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') cmpTelDesc
				,(SELECT cmpTelIIDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CCLN WHERE CCLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CCLN.LanguageID = ' + convert(varchar(10), @LanguageID) + ') cmpTelIIDesc
				,'' '' prsJob
				,'' '' prsPreTel
				,'' '' prsTelUpTo
				,'' '' cnvsStatus '
			/**/
			SET @Joins = @Joins + ' FROM CrmCompany C '
			SET @LanguageCondition = ' 1=1 '
			IF (ISNULL(@cmpSrchCols, '') <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @clmn = @clmn + N' , cmpFT.[rank] AS [CmpRank]'
				SET @OrderBy = @OrderBy + ' CmpRank DESC, '
				/**/
				SET @Joins = @Joins + 'INNER JOIN CONTAINSTABLE(CrmCompany, (' + ISNULL(@cmpSrchCols, '*') + '), @Val' + @JoinsLanguage + ') cmpFT ON C.CompanyID = cmpFT.[Key]'
				/**/
				SET @LanguageCondition = ' CnL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''
				/**/
			END
			/**/
			SET @Joins = @Joins + ' LEFT JOIN CrmProjects Prj ON Prj.prjCompanyId = C.CompanyID '
			/**/
			/**/
			SET @prjSrchAccess = @prjSrchAccess + '
					AND ((Prj.[prjStarterUserId] = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') OR (
	  								(Prj.[prjStarterUserId] <> ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ')
									AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L 
										WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 24
											AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
											AND L.LinkedUserId = Prj.[prjStarterUserId]) > 0
								)
						   ) '
			/**/
		END
		ELSE IF (@SearchType = 5) /*Project*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			Set @clmn = @clmn + '
			,'' '' cnvsSubject
			,'' '' cnvsTopic
			,'' '' cnvsDesc
			,0 ConversationID 
			,'''' cnvsStatus '
			/**/
			SET @Joins = @Joins + ' FROM [CRMvwSelectCompanyAndPersonsAndProjects_Lang] C '
			IF (ISNULL(@prjSrchCols, '') <> '') /*NKR14011118*/
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @Joins = @Joins + ' INNER JOIN CONTAINSTABLE([CRMvwSelectCompanyAndPersonsAndProjects_Lang], (' + ISNULL(@prjSrchCols, '*') + ') , @Val' + @JoinsLanguage + ') prjFTLn ON C.CrmProjectsLangID = prjFTLn.[Key]'
				/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@prjSrchCols, '') <> '') /*NKR14011118*/
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @clmn = @clmn +  N' , prjFTLn.[rank] AS [PrjRank]'
				SET @OrderBy = @OrderBy + ' PrjRank DESC, '
				/**/
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @clmn = @clmn +  N' , 0 AS [PrjRank]'
				/**/
			END
			/**/
			SET @LanguageCondition = ' C.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''
			/**/
			SET @prsSrchAccess = @prsSrchAccess + '
				AND ((C.[FirstRegisterUserId] = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') OR (
						C.[FirstRegisterUserId] <> ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' 
						AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L 
								WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 23
								AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '
								AND L.LinkedUserId = C.FirstRegisterUserId) > 0
						)
					)'
			SET @prjSrchAccess = @prjSrchAccess + '
				AND ((C.[prjStarterUserId] = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') OR (
							C.[prjStarterUserId] <> ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '
							AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L 
									WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 24
									AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '
									AND L.LinkedUserId = C.[prjStarterUserId]) > 0
						)
					)'
			/**/
		END
		/**/
		/**/
		/*NKR14011111- SET @Joins = @Joins + N' WHERE ' + @LanguageCondition*/
		SET @Joins = @Joins + N' WHERE ' + @LanguageCondition + ' ' + @cmpSrchAccess + @prsSrchAccess + @prjSrchAccess + @cnvsSrchAccess
		/*NKR14011125- IF (ISNULL(@cmpSrchConditions, '') <> '') SET @Joins = @Joins + N' OR ' + @cmpSrchConditions*/
		IF (ISNULL(@cmpSrchConditions, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@cmpSrchCols, '') <> '') SET @Joins = @Joins + N' OR ' + @cmpSrchConditions
			ELSE SET @Joins = @Joins + N' AND ' + @cmpSrchConditions
			/**/
		END
		/**/
		IF (ISNULL(@prsSrchConditions, '') <> '') SET @Joins = @Joins + N' OR ' + @prsSrchConditions
		/*NKR14011118- SET @Joins = @Joins + N' AND ' + @prjSrchConditions*/
		IF (ISNULL(@prjSrchConditions, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@prjSrchCols, '') <> '') SET @Joins = @Joins + N' OR ' + @prjSrchConditions
			ELSE SET @Joins = @Joins + N' AND ' + @prjSrchConditions
			/**/
		END
		IF (ISNULL(@cnvsSrchConditions, '') <> '') SET @Joins = @Joins + N' OR ' + @cnvsSrchConditions
		/**/
		/**/
		DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max)
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId
		/**/
		/*NKR14011111- DECLARE @from NVARCHAR(MAX) = @Joins + @cmpSrchAccess + @prsSrchAccess + @prjSrchAccess + @cnvsSrchAccess + @cmpDataPermision*/
		DECLARE @from NVARCHAR(MAX) = @Joins + @cmpDataPermision
		/**/
		SET @from = REPLACE(@from, N'NUM_REP(@Val)', N'N''' + @ValNums + '''')/*NKR14011118*/
		SET @from = REPLACE(@from, N'@Val', N'N''' + @Val + '''')
		/**/
		EXEC (@clmn + @from + ' ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + 'CompanyId DESC, cmpTitle')
		/**/
		DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 10 AND UserId = @UserId
		/**/
		INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo(UserId, TXT, TypeId)
		VALUES (@UserId, @from, 10)
		/**/
	END
	/**/
END






GO

	@Val NVARCHAR(200),
	@UserID INT,
	@SearchType INT,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL, 
	@SearchConditionMode NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE 
		@cmpSrchCols VARCHAR(max) = '',
		@prsSrchCols VARCHAR(max) = '',
		@prjSrchCols VARCHAR(max) = '',
		@cnvsSrchCols VARCHAR(max) = '',
		@cnvsSrchConditions VARCHAR(max) = '',
		/**/
		/**/
		@QRY NVARCHAR(MAX) = N' DECLARE @TBL TABLE(CmpId INT, PrsId INT, PrsFirstRegUserId INT, PrjId INT, CnvsId INT, rnk INT) ',
		@ViewsLang NVARCHAR(100) = N'',
		@ViewsLangWhere NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
		@FullTextLanguage NVARCHAR(300) = N'',
		/*@FullTextLanguage NVARCHAR(300) = N', LANGUAGE N''Arabic''',*/
		@OrderBy NVARCHAR(MAX) = N'',
		@BigTopCnt NVARCHAR(150) = N'800',
		@TopCnt NVARCHAR(150) = N'100',
		@TopCnt30 NVARCHAR(150) = N'30',
		@TopCnt50 NVARCHAR(150) = N'50',
		/**/
		/*ViewsKey*/
		@KeyCrmCompany NVARCHAR(500) = N'CompanyID',
		@KeyCrmCompanyPersons NVARCHAR(500) = N'CompanyPersonID',
		@KeyCrmProjects NVARCHAR(500) = N'ProjectID',
		@KeyCrmConversations NVARCHAR(500) = N'ConversationID',
		/**/
		/*NKR14020530*/
		@LanguageCulture NVARCHAR(50) = (SELECT L.LanguageCollation FROM Languages L WHERE L.LanguageID = @LanguageID)


	IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @ViewsLang = N'_Lang'
		SET @ViewsLangWhere = N'LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(50))

		SET @KeyCrmCompany = N'CrmCompanyLangID'
		SET @KeyCrmCompanyPersons = N'CrmCompanyPersonsLangID'
		SET @KeyCrmProjects = N'CrmProjectsLangID'
		SET @KeyCrmConversations = N'CrmConversationLangID'
		/**/
	END

	SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val, N'''', N'''''')
	SET @Val = '"' + @Val + '"'
	DECLARE @ValNums NVARCHAR(500) = REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(@Val, '*', ''), '"', ''), '''', '')/*NKR14011118*/

	/*Conditions*/
	IF(ISNULL(@SearchConditionMode,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		IF (EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',')
				   WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 1001 OR CAST([value] AS INT) = 1002
				)) /*Star*//*NKR14021103- Add Split Login To @Val*/

			/**/

				ID INT IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,
				StrDate NVARCHAR(300)
			)
			DELETE @SPLITED_TBL

			INSERT INTO @SPLITED_TBL
			SELECT	N'"*' + 
					REPLACE(
						REPLACE(
								REPLACE(
										REPLACE(
											T.[value], 
											N'"', 
											N''
										), 
									N'ي', 
									N'*'
								), 
							N'ك', 
							N'*'
						), 
					N' ',
					N'*')
				+ N'*"'
			 FROM STRING_SPLIT(@Val, ' ') T
			WHERE ISNULL(T.[value], '') <> ''

			SET @Val = ''

			WHILE ((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @SPLITED_TBL T WHERE ISNULL(T.StrDate, '') <> '') > 0)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				DECLARE @NewVal NVARCHAR(200) = (SELECT TOP 1 T.StrDate FROM @SPLITED_TBL T WHERE ISNULL(T.StrDate, '') <> '')
				

				IF (@Val <> '' AND EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 1001))
					SET @Val = @Val + ' AND '
				ELSE IF (@Val <> '' AND EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 1002))
					SET @Val = @Val + ' OR '

				SET @Val = @Val + @NewVal

				UPDATE @SPLITED_TBL SET StrDate = NULL WHERE ID = (SELECT TOP 1 T.ID FROM @SPLITED_TBL T WHERE ISNULL(T.StrDate, '') <> '')
				/**/
			END

			/**/
		END
		ELSE IF (EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 1000)) /*Star*//*NKR14021103- Add Split Login To @Val*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val, N' ', '*')
			SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val, N'ك', N'*')
			SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val, N'ي', N'*')
			SET @Val = REPLACE(@Val, N'"', N'')
			SET @Val = N'"*' +@Val+ N'*"'
			/**/
		END


		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 1)) /*cmpName*/
		BEGIN
			/**/

			SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'cmpName'

			SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'cmpNumberStr'

			/**/
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			/**/

			BEGIN
				/**/

				SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'cmpPreTel'

			BEGIN

				SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'cmpPreTelII'

			BEGIN
				/**/

				SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'cmpTel'
				/**/

		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 10)) /*cmpaddress*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@cmpSrchCols <> '') SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + ','
			SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'cmpAddress'
			/**/
		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 11)) /*CmpCode*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@cmpSrchCols <> '') SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + ','
			SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'CmpCode'

		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 12)) /*cmpNationalCode*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@cmpSrchCols <> '') SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + ','
			SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'cmpNationalCode'
			/**/
		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 13)) /*cmpNameEng*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@cmpSrchCols <> '') SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + ','
			SET @cmpSrchCols = @cmpSrchCols + N'cmpNameEng'
			/**/
		END
		
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 14)) /*prsLName,prsFName*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@prsSrchCols <> '') SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + ','
			SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + N'prsLName,prsFName'
			/**/
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 15)) /*prsLName*/
			BEGIN
				/**/
				IF (@prsSrchCols <> '') SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + ','
				SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + N'prsLName'
				/**/
			END
			IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 16)) /*prsFName*/
			BEGIN
				/**/
				IF (@prsSrchCols <> '') SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + ','
				SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + N'prsFName'
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 17)) /*prsTel,prsTelII*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@prsSrchCols <> '') SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + ','
			SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + N'prsTel,prsTelII'
			/**/
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 18)) /*prsTel*/
			BEGIN
				/**/
				IF (@prsSrchCols <> '') SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + ','
				SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + N'prsTel'
				/**/
			END
			IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 19)) /*prsTelII*/
			BEGIN
				/**/
				IF (@prsSrchCols <> '') SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + ','
				SET @prsSrchCols = @prsSrchCols + N'prsTelII'
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 20)) /*prsMobile*/
		BEGIN

							WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 23
							AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '

				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle' + @ViewsLang + '] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle' + @ViewsLang + '] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '),'''') 
					FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectCompanyPersons' + @ViewsLang + '] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]	' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '')+ '

						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))

			(SELECT City FROM dbo.City' + @ViewsLang + ' WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ') cmpCity,
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM USERS' + @ViewsLang + ' u where u.UserId = C.cmpFirstRegisterUserId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ') cmpFirstRegisterUser,
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM USERS' + @ViewsLang + ' u where u.UserId = C.UserId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ') [EditUser],
			(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users' + @ViewsLang + ' WHERE UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ') cmpReferUser,
			(SELECT ISNULL(U.FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.LName, '''') FROM Users' + @ViewsLang + ' U WHERE U.UserId = C.cmpSuppUserId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ') AS cmpSuppUser,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1
					ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle' + @ViewsLang + ' P WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '), '''') + '' ''+
					ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName, '''') 
				FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectCompanyPersons' + @ViewsLang + ']
				WHERE prsCompanyId = C.CompanyID ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '
				ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID)
			) PersonTop1Order,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 
					RTrim(P.PrjTitle + '' '' + IsNull(prjPostFix, ''''))
				FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectCompanyProjects' + @ViewsLang + '] P 
				WHERE prjCompanyId = C.CompanyID ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '
				  AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished, 0) = 0 
				ORDER BY prjOrderView desc, ProjectId desc
			) PrjTitleTop1Order,
			(
				SELECT TOP 1 ProjectId 
				FROM dbo.CrmProjects P 
				WHERE prjCompanyId = CompanyID 
				  AND isnull(prjIsFinished, 0) = 0 
				ORDER BY prjOrderView desc, ProjectId desc
			) PrjIdTop1Order'
	SET @QRY = @QRY + '
		,(
			SELECT P.[CompanyPersonID],
				   P.[prsLName] AS PrsLName,
				   P.[prsFName] AS PrsFName,
				   ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle' + @ViewsLang + ' PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = P.prsTitleId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '), '''') +
						'' '' + 	ISNULL(P.prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ ISNULL(P.prsFName, '''') PrsName, 
				   P.[prsJobId] AS PrsJobId,

				   P.[prsTitleId] AS PrsTitleId,
				   (SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle' + @ViewsLang + ' PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = P.prsTitleId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ') AS PersonTitle,
				   P.[prsMobile] AS PrsMobile,/*NKR14020705*/
				   P.[prsPreTel] AS PrsPreTel,
				   P.[prsTel] AS PrsTel,
				   P.[prsTelUpTo] AS PrsTelUpTo,
				   P.[prsTelDesc] AS PrsTelDesc
			  FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectCompanyPersons] P 
			 WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = C.cmpMainPersonId
			   FOR JSON PATH
		) AS MainPersonJs
	'
	SET @QRY = @QRY + ',(SELECT GII.Rate FROM CrmCompanyGroupsII GII WHERE GII.CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII) AS cmpGroupIIRate'

				[prsPreTel], [prsTel], [prsTelUpTo], [prsTelDesc], [prsTelII], [prsFax], [prsFaxDesc], [prsMobile], [prsWebSite], [prsEmail], [prsEmailII], 
				[prsCountryId], [prsProvinceId], [prsCityId], [prsAddress], [prsAddressDesc], [prsZipCode], [prsPostBox], [prsNationalCode], [prsIdentityNumber], 
				[prsFatherName], [prsBirthDate], [prsBirthDateTime], [prsBirthPlace], [prsIsMarried], [prsMarriageDate], [prsMarriageDateTime], [prsEducationField], 
				[prsSkill], [prsStudyField], [prsInteresting], [prsSpecialPoint], [prsDescription], [UserId], [Year1], [Month1], [RegisterDate], [RegisterDatePersian],
				[AddType], [ImportDate], [ImportDateTime], [prsFirstRegisterUserId], [prsDepartmentId], [prsStopSendSms], [prsOrderView], [prsWorkingOut], [GUId], 
				[CompanyPersonTemplateID], [AppType], [LanguageID], [FirstRegisterUserId], [FirstRegisterDatePersian], [prsTelegramId], [FirstRegisterDate], 
				[prsCoordinateLatitude], [prsCoordinateLongitude], [prsCoordinateLastDate], [prsCoordinateLastDatePersian], [prsCoordinateZoomLevel], [prsCoordinateLastUserId], 
				(
					ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle' + @ViewsLang + ' PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = P.prsTitleId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '), '''') 
						+ '' '' + ISNULL(P.prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ ISNULL(P.prsFName, '''')
				) AS prsName,
				(SELECT ISNULL(PJ.Jobs, '''') FROM CrmPersonJobs' + @ViewsLang + ' PJ WHERE PJ.PersonJobID = P.prsJobId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ') prsJob,
				/*ISNULL((SELECT CAST(''1'' AS BIT) FROM @TBL T WHERE T.CmpId = C.CompanyID AND T.PrsId = P.CompanyPersonID), CAST(''0'' AS BIT)) AS IsExistInSearch*/

				ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) CAST(''1'' AS BIT) FROM @TBL T WHERE T.CmpId = C.CompanyID AND T.PrsId = P.CompanyPersonID), CAST(''0'' AS BIT)) AS IsExistInSearch/*RZ14020302- Modify in type 3*/ 
			FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectCompanyPersons' + @ViewsLang + '] P 
			WHERE P.[prsCompanyId] = C.CompanyID ' + ISNULL('AND P.' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') 
	SET @QRY = @QRY + ' 
				AND ((ISNULL(P.FirstRegisterUserId, ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') = ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')
					OR ((P.FirstRegisterUserId <> ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')
					AND
						(SELECT COUNT (*)
						FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L
						WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 23
						AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '
						AND L.LinkedUserId = P.FirstRegisterUserId ) > 0))'
	SET @QRY = @QRY + ' ORDER BY ISNULL(P.prsOrderView, P.CompanyPersonID) '	/*RZ14020224- Add order by*/
	SET @QRY = @QRY + '
			FOR JSON PATH
		) AS PRS '
	/**/
	/*PRJ*/
	DECLARE @PRJ NVARCHAR(MAX) = N''
	IF (@SearchType = 1)/*قبلا از تاپ 1 استفاده میشد الان 100 تای آخر میان*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @PRJ = N'
			,(
				SELECT TOP ' + @TopCnt + '
					[ProjectID], [prjCompanyId], [prjTitle], [prjDesc], [prjStartDate], [prjStartDay], [prjStartTime], [prjStartDateTime], [prjStarterUserId], 
					[prjFinishedStatusId], [prjIsFinished], [prjFinishedDate], [prjFinishedDay], [prjFinishedTime], [prjFinishedDateTime], [prjFinishedUserId], 
					[prjGradeId], [prjPriorityId], [prjFirstRegisterDate], [prjFirstRegisterUserId], [UserId], [Year1], [Month1], [RegisterDate], 
					[RegisterDatePersian], [GUId], [prjGroupId], [prjcnvsStatusGroupId], [prjOrderView], [AppType], [prjPostFix], [LanguageID], [FirstRegisterUserId], 
					[FirstRegisterDateTime], [FirstRegisterDatePersian], [prjFirstRegisterDateTime], [FirstRegisterDate], [prjRemindDate], [prjRemindDateTime], 
					[prjFOCmpCanSee], [prjIsTicket], [prjUserDepartmentId], [prjHaveSms], [prjSmsPrsIds], [prjSmsText], [prjSmsSendDate], [prjSmsSendDatePersian], 
					[prjInquiryNum], [prjSubject], [prjTitleGroup], [prjCategoryId], [prjDescI], [prjNumRef], [prjNumR], [prjNumRStr], [prjToDoTime], 
					[prjToDoTimeRegister], [prjToDoTimeDone], [prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs], [prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs], [prjTicketStop], [prjIsTicketNew], [prjToDoTimeSec], 
					[prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory], [prjReadDate], [prjReadUserId], [prjReadDuration], [prjResponseDate], [prjResponseUserId], [prjResponseDuration], 
					[prjFirstDoingDate], [prjFirstDoingUserId], [prjFirstDoingDuration], [prjFinishedDuration], [prjTicketStopUserId], [prjTicketStopRegisterDate], 
					[prjTicketStopRegisterDatePersian], [prjcmpPresentationId], [prjcmpCampaignIds]
				FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectCompanyProjects' + @ViewsLang + '] PRJ
				WHERE PRJ.prjCompanyId = C.CompanyID
				  AND ISNULL(PRJ.prjIsFinished, 0) = 0
				  AND ISNULL(PRJ.prjIsTicket, 0) = 0
				  ' + ISNULL(' AND PRJ.' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '

				  /*RZ14020224- Add order by*/
				  AND ((PRJ.[prjStarterUserId] = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') OR (
							PRJ.[prjStarterUserId] <> ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '
							AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L 
									WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 24
									AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '
									AND L.LinkedUserId = PRJ.[prjStarterUserId]) > 0
						))
				  /*RZ14020224- End*/

				ORDER BY prjOrderView desc, ProjectId desc
				FOR JSON PATH
			) AS PRJ
		'
		/**/
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @PRJ = '
				,(
					SELECT TOP ' + @TopCnt + '
						[ProjectID], [prjCompanyId], [prjTitle], [prjDesc], [prjStartDate], [prjStartDay], [prjStartTime], [prjStartDateTime], [prjStarterUserId], 
						[prjFinishedStatusId], [prjIsFinished], [prjFinishedDate], [prjFinishedDay], [prjFinishedTime], [prjFinishedDateTime], [prjFinishedUserId], 
						[prjGradeId], [prjPriorityId], [prjFirstRegisterDate], [prjFirstRegisterUserId], [UserId], [Year1], [Month1], [RegisterDate], 
						[RegisterDatePersian], [GUId], [prjGroupId], [prjcnvsStatusGroupId], [prjOrderView], [AppType], [prjPostFix], [LanguageID], [FirstRegisterUserId], 
						[FirstRegisterDateTime], [FirstRegisterDatePersian], [prjFirstRegisterDateTime], [FirstRegisterDate], [prjRemindDate], [prjRemindDateTime], 
						[prjFOCmpCanSee], [prjIsTicket], [prjUserDepartmentId], [prjHaveSms], [prjSmsPrsIds], [prjSmsText], [prjSmsSendDate], [prjSmsSendDatePersian], 
						[prjInquiryNum], [prjSubject], [prjTitleGroup], [prjCategoryId], [prjDescI], [prjNumRef], [prjNumR], [prjNumRStr], [prjToDoTime], 
						[prjToDoTimeRegister], [prjToDoTimeDone], [prjFOCmpCanInsCnvs], [prjFOCmpCanNotInsCnvs], [prjTicketStop], [prjIsTicketNew], [prjToDoTimeSec], 
						[prjToDoTimeRegisterHistory], [prjReadDate], [prjReadUserId], [prjReadDuration], [prjResponseDate], [prjResponseUserId], [prjResponseDuration], 
						[prjFirstDoingDate], [prjFirstDoingUserId], [prjFirstDoingDuration], [prjFinishedDuration], [prjTicketStopUserId], [prjTicketStopRegisterDate], 
						[prjTicketStopRegisterDatePersian], [prjcmpPresentationId], [prjcmpCampaignIds]
					FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectCompanyProjects' + @ViewsLang + '] PRJ
					WHERE PRJ.ProjectID IN (SELECT T.PrjId FROM @TBL T WHERE T.CmpId = C.CompanyID) ' + ISNULL(' AND PRJ.' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '
					AND ((' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' IN (SELECT P.FirstRegisterUserId FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.prsCompanyId = PRJ.prjCompanyId)) OR (
						' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' NOT IN (SELECT P.FirstRegisterUserId FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.prsCompanyId = PRJ.prjCompanyId) 
						AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L 
								WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 23
								AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '
								AND L.LinkedUserId IN (SELECT P.FirstRegisterUserId FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE P.prsCompanyId = PRJ.prjCompanyId)) > 0
						)
					)
					AND ((PRJ.[prjStarterUserId] = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + ') OR (
							PRJ.[prjStarterUserId] <> ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '
							AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L 
									WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 24
									AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '
									AND L.LinkedUserId = PRJ.[prjStarterUserId]) > 0
						)
					)
					FOR JSON PATH
				) AS PRJ '
		/**/
	END
	SET @QRY = @QRY + @PRJ
	/**/
	/*CNVS*/
	SET @QRY = @QRY + '
			/*CNVS*/
			,(
				SELECT TOP ' + @TopCnt + '
					[ConversationID], [cnvsProjectId], [cnvsCompanyId], [cnvsDay], [cnvsTime], [cnvsTopicId], [cnvsSubject], [cnvsDesc], [cnvsPersonId], [cnvsUserId], 
					[cnvsReferUserId], [cnvsReferDesc], [cnvsStatusId], [cnvsPriorityId], [cnvsRemindDate], [cnvsRemindTime], [cnvsRemindOk], [cnvsIsBookMark], [cnvsFirstRegisterUserId], 
					[cnvsFirstRegisterDate], [UserId], [Year1], [Month1], [RegisterDate], [RegisterDatePersian], [FirstRegisterDate], [GUId], [cnvsRemindDateForAllarm], [AppType], 
					[cnvsDateTime], [cnvsRemindDateTime], [cnvsFirstRegisterDateTime], [cnvsRemindDateTimeForAllarm], [LanguageID], [FirstRegisterUserId], [FirstRegisterDatePersian], 
					[ApiId], [cnvsFOCmpCanSee], [cnvsIsTicket], [cnvsCoordinateLatitude], [cnvsCoordinateLongitude], [cnvsCoordinateLastDate], [cnvsCoordinateLastDatePersian], [cnvsTicketIsRead], 
					[cnvsDoingTime], [cnvsIsCopy], [cnvsIsTicketNew], [cnvsDoingTimeSec], [cnvsStatusProcessKindId], [cnvsStatusGroupId], [cnvsHappyVote],
					(select CS.StatusColor from CrmConversationStatus CS WHERE CS.ConversationStatusID = CNVS.cnvsStatusId) AS StatusColor,
					(FORMAT(cnvsDateTime, ''yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss'', ''' + @LanguageCulture + ''')) AS [cnvsDate]
				FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectConversation' + @ViewsLang + '] CNVS
				WHERE CNVS.ConversationID IN (SELECT T.CnvsId FROM @TBL T WHERE T.CmpId = C.CompanyID) ' + ISNULL(' AND CNVS.' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '
				FOR JSON PATH
			) AS CNVS'
	/**/
	/*FROM*/
	SET @QRY = @QRY + '
		FROM [dbo].[CRMvwSelectCompany' + @ViewsLang + '] C
		WHERE C.CompanyID IN (SELECT T.CmpId FROM @TBL T) ' + ISNULL('AND C.' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ' '
		
	EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
	DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max) = (SELECT TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @UserId)
	IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(@cmpDataPermision, ''))) <> '') SET @QRY = @QRY + N' ' + @cmpDataPermision

	SET @QRY = REPLACE(@QRY, N'@Val', N'N''' + @Val + '''')
	
	
	EXEC(@QRY)
	/*EXEC (@QRY + ' ORDER BY ' + @OrderBy + ' CompanyId DESC, cmpTitle')*/
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspResultsForgetCompanyCartable] | References permission/temp-filter machinery






/*ADBF93039408*/		
/*RZ981105- Add prjPostFix*/
/*ADBF990126- Add Param*/
/*RZ990504- Add linkedUser*/
/*RZ14000616- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*RZ14010508- Change condition from >0*/
/*NKR14020507- Add CountryDivision Access*/
/*NKR14020928- Use DateTime Instead String*/
/*NKR14021017- Add Type Cast For DateTime*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspResultsForgetCompanyCartable]
(
	/*
		exec CRMspResultsForgetCompanyCartable '13930113', '13930313', '', '', 12, '', 59, 0, 1, 3, '1393/03/23', 'asd'  
	*/
	@FDate VARCHAR(20), 
	@TDate VARCHAR(20),
	@FDateTime DATETIME, 
	@TDateTime DATETIME,
	@cnvsStatusId VARCHAR(1000), 
	@prjTitle NVARCHAR(500), 
	@cnvsuserId VARCHAR(1000), 
	@cnvsReferUserId VARCHAR(1000),
	@NoRemindDate BIT,
	@rptUserID INT,
	@rptDate VARCHAR(15),
	@rptDay NVARCHAR(20),
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
	/*ADBF990126*/
	,@prjPostFix NVARCHAR(500) = Null 
)
AS
BEGIN 
	/**/
	if(@LanguageID is null or @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	begin
		/*
		DECLARE @FDate VARCHAR(20), @TDate VARCHAR(20)
		SET @FDate = '13930113'
		SET @TDate = '13930313'
		*/
		DECLARE @Term NVARCHAR(max), @cmpDataPermision NVARCHAR(MAX) = N''
		SET @Term = ''
		/*------------------------------------*/
		IF @cnvsStatusId <> 0 /*RZ14010508- Change condition from >0*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvs.cnvsStatusId IN ( ' + @cnvsStatusId + ')'
		/*------------------------------------*/
		IF @prjTitle <> ''
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( SELECT prjTitle FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE ProjectID = 
			cnvs.cnvsProjectId ) IN ( N''' + @prjTitle + ''')'
		/*------------------------------------*/
		IF @cnvsuserId <> 0 /*RZ14010508- Change condition from >0*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvs.cnvsuserId IN ( ' + @cnvsuserId + ')'
		/*------------------------------------*/
		IF @cnvsReferUserId <> 0/*RZ14010508- Change condition from >0*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvsReferUserId IN ( ' + @cnvsReferUserId + ')'
		/*------------------------------------*/
		IF @NoRemindDate = 1
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( cnvsRemindDate IS NULL OR ISNULL(cnvsRemindDate, '''') = '''')'
		/*------------------------------------*/
		/*ADBF990126-B*/
		IF @prjPostFix <> ''
		SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( SELECT prjPostFix FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE ProjectID = 
			cnvs.cnvsProjectId ) IN ( N''' + @prjPostFix + ''')'
		/*ADBF990126-E*/
		/*------------------------------------*/
		SET @Term = @Term + ' ORDER BY  ImportDate , cnvs.cnvsDate DESC '
		/*------------------------------------*/

		/*NKR14020507- Start*/
	
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @rptUserID
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @rptUserID

		/*NKR14020507- End*/

		/*NKR14021017- Start*/
		SET @FDate = CAST(CAST(@FDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50))
		SET @TDate = CAST(CAST(@TDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50))
		/*NKR14021017- End*/


		EXECUTE(
		'
		SELECT DISTINCT ' + @FDate + ' FDate, ' + @TDate + ' TDate, '+ 
		' ( SELECT ISNULL( FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( LName, '''')
		  FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = ' + @rptUserId + ' ) rptUser, ''' +
		@rptDate + ''' rptDate, ''' + @rptDay + ''' rptDay, ' +  
		/*' ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle 
		WHERE CompanyTitleId = cmpTitleId), '''') + '' '' + cmpName Company, '*/

		/*RZ14000616- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
		' (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)Company,' + 
		/*RZ14000616- End*/			


		' ( SELECT ISNULL(prjTitle,'''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prjPostFix,'''') FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE ProjectID =  cnvsProjectId) prjTitle, /*RZ981105- Add prjPostFix*/
		cnvsDate,
		cnvsDateTime,
		ISNULL(( SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleID =
		( SELECT prsTitleId FROM CrmCompanyPersons WHERE 
		CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId )), '''') + '' '' +
		(SELECT ISNULL( prsLName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( prsFName, '''')
		FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId ) cnvsPerson,
		( SELECT ISNULL( LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = cnvs.cnvsUserId) cnvsUser,
		( SELECT ISNULL( LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = cnvs.cnvsReferUserId) cnvsReferUser,  
		(SELECT Status FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatus WHERE ConversationStatusId = cnvsStatusId) cnvsStatus, 
		cnvsDesc, 
		( select Presentation from dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation WHERE CompanyPresentationID = cmpPresentationId) CompanyPresentation, 
		cnvsRemindDate,
		cnvsRemindDateTime,  
		CompanyId, 
		cnvsPersonId, 
		cnvsProjectId, 
		ConversationId, 
		ImportDate,
		ImportDateTime, 
		cnvsRemindOk, 
		(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceID = cmpProvinceId ) cmpProvince,
		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = cmpCityId ) cmpCity,' +
		'''' + @FDateTime + ''' FDateTime, ' +
		'''' + @TDateTime + ''' TDateTime 
		/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
		FROM dbo.CrmCompany C INNER JOIN CrmConversation cnvs
			ON ( C.CompanyID = cnvs.cnvsCompanyId)
			AND cnvs.ConversationID = (SELECT TOP 1 ConversationID
						FROM dbo.CrmConversation con
						WHERE con.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
						ORDER  BY con.cnvsDate DESC )
		WHERE (
		(
		 (SELECT TOP 1 CAST(cnvsDateTime AS DATE) 
			FROM dbo.CrmConversation con
			WHERE con.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
			ORDER  BY con.cnvsDateTime DESC ) NOT BETWEEN ''' + /*@FDate*/ @FDate + ''' AND ''' + /*@TDate*/ @TDate + '''' + 
			' OR (SELECT TOP 1 cnvsDate 
			FROM dbo.CrmConversation con2
			WHERE con2.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
			ORDER  BY con2.cnvsDate DESC ) IS NULL
			) 

			)
			/*RZ990504- Add linkedUser*/
			AND	(SELECT TOP 1 CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId) IN 
				(SELECT '+ @rptUserID + ' UNION 
				SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ @rptUserID + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
			AND	(SELECT TOP 1 PR.[prjStarterUserId] FROM dbo.CrmProjects PR WHERE PR.ProjectID = cnvs.cnvsProjectId) IN 
				(SELECT '+ @rptUserID + ' UNION 
				SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ @rptUserID + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24)
			AND (
				(cnvs.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
				(SELECT '+ @rptUserID + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ @rptUserID + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))
				OR (cnvs.[cnvsUserId] IN 
				(SELECT '+ @rptUserID + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ @rptUserID + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))
				OR (cnvs.[cnvsReferUserId] IN 
				(SELECT '+ @rptUserID + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ @rptUserID + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))				
			)
			/*RZ990504- End*/
			' + @cmpDataPermision + '
		' + @Term )
	end
	else if(@LanguageID is not null and @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	begin
		EXEC CRMspResultsForgetCompanyCartable_Lang 
		@FDate, 
		@TDate,
		@FDateTime, 
		@TDateTime,
		@cnvsStatusId, 
		@prjTitle, 
		@cnvsuserId, 
		@cnvsReferUserId,
		@NoRemindDate,
		@rptUserID,
		@rptDate,
		@rptDay,
		@LanguageID,
		@DefaultLanguageID
		/*ADBF990126*/
		,@prjPostFix
	end
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[CRMspResultsForgetCompanyCartableWeb] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*ADBF93039408*/		
/*RZ981105- Add prjPostFix*/
/*ADBF990126- Add Param*/
/*RZ990504- Add linkedUser*/
/*RZ14000616- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*RZ14010508- Change condition from > 0*/
/*NKR14020507- Add CountryDivision Access*/
/*NKR14020928- Use DateTime Instead String*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspResultsForgetCompanyCartableWeb]
(
	/*
		exec [dbo].[CRMspResultsForgetCompanyCartableWeb]
			@FDate='1401/12/01',@TDate='1402/06/28',@FDateTime='2023-02-20 00:00:00',@TDateTime='2023-09-19 00:00:00',
			@cnvsStatusId='',@prjTitle=N'',@cnvsuserId='',@cnvsReferUserId='',@NoRemindDate=0,@rptUserID=3,@rptDate='1402/06/28',@rptDay=N'سه شنبه',
			@LanguageID=1065,@DefaultLanguageID=1065,@prjPostFix=N'',@LastRowNumber=100,@PageSize=100,@IsPrint=0
	*/
	@FDate VARCHAR(20), 
	@TDate VARCHAR(20),
	@FDateTime DATETIME, 
	@TDateTime DATETIME,
	@cnvsStatusId VARCHAR(1000), 
	@prjTitle NVARCHAR(500), 
	@cnvsuserId VARCHAR(1000), 
	@cnvsReferUserId VARCHAR(1000),
	@NoRemindDate BIT,
	@rptUserID INT,
	@rptDate VARCHAR(15),
	@rptDay NVARCHAR(20),
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
	@prjPostFix NVARCHAR(500) = Null,
	@LastRowNumber INT = NULL,
	@PageSize INT = NULL,
	@IsPrint BIT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN 
	/**/
	DECLARE @Term NVARCHAR(max), @cmpDataPermision NVARCHAR(MAX) = N'', @QRY NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
	SET @Term = ''
	IF (ISNULL(@IsPrint, 0) = 1) SET @PageSize = NULL
	/**/
	IF (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		IF (@cnvsStatusId <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvs.cnvsStatusId IN ( ' + @cnvsStatusId + ')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@prjTitle <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( SELECT prjTitle FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE ProjectID = 
				cnvs.cnvsProjectId ) IN ( N''' + @prjTitle + ''')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@cnvsuserId <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvs.cnvsuserId IN ( ' + @cnvsuserId + ')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@cnvsReferUserId <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvsReferUserId IN ( ' + @cnvsReferUserId + ')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@NoRemindDate = 1)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( cnvsRemindDate IS NULL OR ISNULL(cnvsRemindDate, '''') = '''')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@prjPostFix <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( SELECT prjPostFix FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE ProjectID = 
				cnvs.cnvsProjectId ) IN ( N''' + @prjPostFix + ''')'
			/**/
		END
	
		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @rptUserID
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @rptUserID


		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' SELECT '
		IF (ISNULL(@PageSize, 0) <> 0) SET @QRY = @QRY + ' TOP ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50))
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' * FROM ('
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' SELECT DISTINCT'
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' ' + @FDate + ' FDate, 
							' + @TDate + ' TDate,'+ 
							'(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY ImportDate, cnvsDate DESC)) AS RowNum,' + 
							'( SELECT ISNULL( FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' ) rptUser, 
							''' + @rptDate + ''' rptDate, 
							''' + @rptDay + ''' rptDay,
							(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
								THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
										ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
								ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
											ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
									WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
									)				
							END)Company,
							( SELECT ISNULL(prjTitle,'''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prjPostFix,'''') FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE ProjectID =  cnvsProjectId) prjTitle,
							cnvsDate,
							cnvsDateTime,
							ISNULL(( SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersonTitleID =
							( SELECT prsTitleId FROM CrmCompanyPersons WHERE 
							CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId )), '''') + '' '' +
							(SELECT ISNULL( prsLName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( prsFName, '''')
							FROM CrmCompanyPersons P WHERE CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId ) cnvsPerson,
							( SELECT ISNULL( LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = cnvs.cnvsUserId) cnvsUser,
							( SELECT ISNULL( LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId = cnvs.cnvsReferUserId) cnvsReferUser,  
							(SELECT Status FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatus WHERE ConversationStatusId = cnvsStatusId) cnvsStatus, 
							cnvsDesc, 
							( select Presentation from dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation WHERE CompanyPresentationID = cmpPresentationId) CompanyPresentation, 
							cnvsRemindDate,
							cnvsRemindDateTime,  
							CompanyId, 
							cnvsPersonId, 
							cnvsProjectId, 
							ConversationId, 
							ImportDate,
							ImportDateTime, 
							cnvsRemindOk, 
							(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceID = cmpProvinceId ) cmpProvince,
							(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityID = cmpCityId ) cmpCity,
							''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS NVARCHAR(100)) + ''' FDateTime, 
							''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS NVARCHAR(100)) + ''' TDateTime 
							FROM dbo.CrmCompany C INNER JOIN CrmConversation cnvs
								ON ( C.CompanyID = cnvs.cnvsCompanyId)
								AND cnvs.ConversationID = (SELECT TOP 1 ConversationID
											FROM dbo.CrmConversation con
											WHERE con.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
											ORDER  BY con.cnvsDate DESC )'

		SET @QRY = @QRY + '
			WHERE (
				(
					(
						SELECT TOP 1 /*NKR14020928- cnvsDate*/CAST(cnvsDateTime AS DATE)
						FROM dbo.CrmConversation con
						WHERE con.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
						ORDER  BY con.cnvsDateTime /*NKR14020928- con.cnvsDate*/ DESC ) NOT BETWEEN ''' + /*NKR14020928- @FDate*/ CAST(CAST(@FDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + /*NKR14020928- @TDate*/ CAST(CAST(@TDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) + '''' + 
						' OR (SELECT TOP 1 cnvsDate 
						FROM dbo.CrmConversation con2
						WHERE con2.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
						ORDER  BY con2.cnvsDate DESC ) IS NULL
					) 
				)
				AND	(SELECT TOP 1 CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId) IN 
					(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
				AND	(SELECT TOP 1 PR.[prjStarterUserId] FROM dbo.CrmProjects PR WHERE PR.ProjectID = cnvs.cnvsProjectId) IN 
					(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24)
				AND (
					(cnvs.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
					(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
						SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))
					OR (cnvs.[cnvsUserId] IN 
					(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
						SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))
					OR (cnvs.[cnvsReferUserId] IN 
					(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
						SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))				
				)
				' + @cmpDataPermision + '
			' + @Term

		SET @QRY = @QRY + ') AS TMP WHERE 1=1 '

		IF (ISNULL(@LastRowNumber, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@IsPrint, 0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @QRY = @QRY + 'AND (
										(' + CAST(@LastRowNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' <= 0)
										OR (' + CAST(@LastRowNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' > 0 AND (TMP.RowNum > ' + CAST(@LastRowNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '))
								   )'
				/**/
			END
			ELSE IF (ISNULL(@IsPrint, 0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @QRY = @QRY + 'AND (TMP.RowNum <= ' + CAST(@LastRowNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')'
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		END

		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' ORDER BY ImportDate, cnvsDate DESC '

		EXEC(@QRY)
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		IF (@cnvsStatusId <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvs.cnvsStatusId IN ( ' + @cnvsStatusId + ')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@prjTitle <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( SELECT prjTitle FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang POLN WHERE POLN.ProjectID = cnvs.cnvsProjectId AND POLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID as varchar(max)) + ' ) IN ( N''' + @prjTitle + ''')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@cnvsuserId <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvs.cnvsuserId IN ( ' + @cnvsuserId + ')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@cnvsReferUserId <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND cnvsReferUserId IN ( ' + @cnvsReferUserId + ')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@NoRemindDate = 1)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( cnvsRemindDateTime IS NULL OR ISNULL(cnvsRemindDateTime, '''') = '''')'
			/**/
		END
	
		IF (@prjPostFix <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + ' AND ( SELECT prjPostFix FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang POLN WHERE POLN.ProjectID = cnvs.cnvsProjectId AND POLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID as varchar(max)) + ' ) IN ( N''' + @prjPostFix + ''')'
			/**/
		END
	
		declare @StrLanguageID varchar(max) = cast(@LanguageID as varchar(max))
		declare @strRptUserId varchar(max) = cast(@rptUserId as varchar(max))

		EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @rptUserID
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 20 AND UserId = @rptUserID

		declare @strFDateTime varchar(max) = cast(@FDateTime as varchar(max))
		declare @strTDateTime varchar(max) = cast(@TDateTime as varchar(max))
		/**/
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' SELECT '
		IF (ISNULL(@PageSize, 0) <> 0) SET @QRY = @QRY + ' TOP ' + CAST(@PageSize AS NVARCHAR(50))
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' * FROM ('
		SET @QRY = @QRY + 'SELECT DISTINCT '
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' ' + @FDate + ' FDate,
							' + @TDate + ' TDate,' +
							'(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY ImportDate, cnvsDate DESC)) AS RowNum,' + 
							'(SELECT ISNULL( FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( LName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = ' + @strRptUserId + ' AND ULN.LanguageID =  ' + @StrLanguageID + ' ) rptUser, 
							''' +@rptDate + ''' rptDate, ''' + @rptDay + ''' rptDay, 
							(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
								THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + '),'''') + '' '' + 
										ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + '),'''')))
								ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + '),'''')  + '' '' +
											ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + '),'''')
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
									WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
									)				
							END)Company,'
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' (SELECT ISNULL(prjTitle,'''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prjPostFix,'''') FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang P WHERE P.ProjectID = cnvs.cnvsProjectId AND P.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ') prjTitle,/*RZ981105- Add prjPostFix*/
							cnvsDate,
							cnvsDateTime,
							ISNULL(( SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang CPPLN WHERE CPPLN.PersonTitleID = (SELECT prsTitleId FROM CrmCompanyPersons WHERE CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId) AND CPPLN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ' ), '''') + '' '' +
							(SELECT ISNULL( prsLName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( prsFName, '''') FROM CrmCompanyPersons_Lang P WHERE P.CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId AND P.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ' ) cnvsPerson,
							(SELECT ISNULL( LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = cnvs.cnvsUserId AND ULN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ') cnvsUser,
							(SELECT ISNULL( LName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL( FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = cnvs.cnvsReferUserId AND ULN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ') cnvsReferUser,  
							(SELECT Status FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatus_Lang CSTLN WHERE CSTLN.ConversationStatusId = cnvs.cnvsStatusId AND CSTLN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ') cnvsStatus, '
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' (SELECT cnvsDesc FROM CrmConversation_Lang CONVLN where CONVLN.ConversationID = cnvs.ConversationID AND CONVLN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ') cnvsDesc, 
							(select Presentation from dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation_Lang PERLN WHERE PERLN.CompanyPresentationID = cmpPresentationId AND PERLN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ') CompanyPresentation, 
							cnvsRemindDate,  
							cnvsRemindDateTime,
							CompanyId, 
							cnvsPersonId, 
							cnvsProjectId, 
							ConversationId, 
							ImportDate, 
							ImportDateTime,
							cnvsRemindOk,'
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.ProvinceID = cmpProvinceId AND PLN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ') cmpProvince,
							(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CYLN WHERE CYLN.CityID = cmpCityId AND CYLN.LanguageID = ' + @StrLanguageID + ') cmpCity, 
							''' + @strFDateTime + ''' FDateTime,
							''' + @strTDateTime + ''' TDateTime
							FROM dbo.CrmCompany C INNER JOIN CrmConversation cnvs
								ON ( C.CompanyID = cnvs.cnvsCompanyId)
								AND cnvs.ConversationID = (SELECT TOP 1 ConversationID
											FROM dbo.CrmConversation con
											WHERE con.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
											ORDER BY con.cnvsDate DESC )'
		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' 
					WHERE (
						(
							(SELECT TOP 1 /*cnvsDateTime*/ CAST(cnvsDateTime AS DATE)
								FROM dbo.CrmConversation con
								WHERE con.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
								ORDER  BY con.cnvsDateTime DESC ) NOT BETWEEN ''' + /*@strFDateTime*/ CAST(CAST(@FDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) +''' AND ''' + /*@strTDateTime*/ CAST(CAST(@TDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) +''' OR (SELECT TOP 1 cnvsDateTime 
								FROM dbo.CrmConversation con2
								WHERE con2.cnvsCompanyId = C.CompanyId
								ORDER  BY con2.cnvsDateTime DESC ) IS NULL
							)
						)
						AND	(SELECT TOP 1 CP.[FirstRegisterUserId] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = cnvs.cnvsPersonId) IN 
							(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
						AND	(SELECT TOP 1 PR.[prjStarterUserId] FROM dbo.CrmProjects PR WHERE PR.ProjectID = cnvs.cnvsProjectId) IN 
							(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
							SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24)
						AND (
							(cnvs.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
							(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))
							OR (cnvs.[cnvsUserId] IN 
							(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))
							OR (cnvs.[cnvsReferUserId] IN 
							(SELECT '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' UNION 
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ CAST(@rptUserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))				
						)
						/*RZ990504- End*/
						' + @cmpDataPermision + '
					' + @Term

		SET @QRY = @QRY + ') AS TMP WHERE 1=1 '

		IF (ISNULL(@LastRowNumber, 0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@IsPrint, 0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @QRY = @QRY + 'AND (
										(' + CAST(@LastRowNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' <= 0)
										OR (' + CAST(@LastRowNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' > 0 AND (TMP.RowNum > ' + CAST(@LastRowNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '))
								   )'
				/**/
			END
			ELSE IF (ISNULL(@IsPrint, 0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @QRY = @QRY + 'AND (TMP.RowNum <= ' + CAST(@LastRowNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')'
				/**/
			END
			/**/
		END

		SET @QRY = @QRY + ' ORDER BY ImportDate, cnvsDate DESC '
		
		EXEC(@QRY)
		/**/
	END
	/**/
END








GO

	@FormId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@FieldId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@Values NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@rptUserId INT,
	@FromFirstRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@ToFirstRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@FromRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@ToRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@CompanyId NVARCHAR(4000),
	@FirstRegisterUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@RegisterUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@CheckedStatusId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
	@MasterValueId INT = NULL,
	@FromCheckedDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@ToCheckedDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@CheckerUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@DyFormNumber NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@VerificationWorkflowId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@VerificationWorkflowSetpId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@SelectedFieldsIds NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/

	DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '', 
			@Term VARCHAR(MAX) = '',
			@BeforeQry NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''


	/*Create Form Temp Table*/

		DECLARE @Forms_tbl TABLE(
			[FormId] [int] NULL,
			[FormName] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[FormTitle] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[FormTitleColor] [nvarchar](50) NULL,

			[UploadFileDescription] [nvarchar](4000) NULL,

			[UploadFileMaxSize] [int] NULL,

			[FOSysPartId] [int] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterUserId] [int] NULL,
