			[FirstRegisterDatePersian] [varchar](8) NULL,
			[UserId] [int] NULL,
			[RegisterDate] [datetime] NULL,
			[RegisterDatePersian] [nvarchar](8) NULL,
			[Year1] [smallint] NULL,
			[Month1] [smallint] NULL,
			[GUId] [uniqueidentifier] NULL,
			[AppType] [varchar](20) NULL,
			[LanguageID] [int] NULL,
			[UploadFileExtension] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[PrintFormatId] [int] NULL,
			[FOIsVisible] [bit] NULL,
			[FOIsEnable] [bit] NULL,
			[FOCanInsertMulti] [bit] NULL,
			[EnableForUsersId] [nvarchar](4000) NULL,
			[EnableForRollId] [int] NULL,
			[CheckerUsersId] [nvarchar](4000) NULL,
			[WorkflowId] [int] NULL,
			[CrmSysPartShow] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[FOSysPartShow] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[SetSysPartFieldByUser] [bit] NULL,
			[EnableForResponsibleUserChecker] [bit] NULL,
			[VerificationWorkFlowId] [int] NULL
		)
		'



	/*Create Form Field Temp Table*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
		DECLARE @FormFields_tbl TABLE (
			[FormFieldId] [int] NULL,
			[ParentFormId] [int] NULL,
			[FormId] [int] NULL,
			[FieldTypeId] [int] NULL,
			[ParentFieldId] [int] NULL,
			[ParentFieldColor] [nvarchar](50) NULL,
			[ParentFieldBgColor] [nvarchar](50) NULL,
			[FieldTitle] [nvarchar](max) NULL,
			[IsRequired] [bit] NULL,
			[RegularExpressionId] [int] NULL,
			[RegularExpressionErrorTxt] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[MinValue] [bigint] NULL,
			[MaxValue] [bigint] NULL,
			[StrLength] [bigint] NULL,
			[PlaceholderTxt] [nvarchar](4000) NULL,
			[MultiItems] [nvarchar](max) NULL,
			[ChartItemsJs] [nvarchar](max) NULL,
			[DefaultValue] [nvarchar](max) NULL,
			[IsActive] [bit] NULL,
			[ColumnPosition] [int] NULL,
			[OrderView] [int] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterUserId] [int] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterDate] [datetime] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterDatePersian] [varchar](8) NULL,
			[UserId] [int] NULL,
			[RegisterDate] [datetime] NULL,
			[RegisterDatePersian] [nvarchar](8) NULL,
			[Year1] [smallint] NULL,
			[Month1] [smallint] NULL,
			[GUId] [uniqueidentifier] NULL,
			[AppType] [varchar](20) NULL,
			[LanguageID] [int] NULL,
			[RowPosition] [int] NULL,
			[IsRepeatable] [bit] NULL,
			[FOIsRepeatable] [bit] NULL,
			[UploadFileMaxSize] [int] NULL,
			[UploadFileExtension] [nvarchar](1000) NULL
		)
		'



	/*Create Field Values Temp Table*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
		DECLARE @FormFieldValues_tbl TABLE (
			VAL_FormId INT, 
			VAL_FieldId INT, 
			VAL_Value NVARCHAR(MAX),
			VAL_CompanyId INT,
			VAL_CompanyPersonId INT,
			VAL_ProjectId INT,
			VAL_CnvsId INT,
			VAL_FirstRegisterUserId INT,
			VAL_FirstRegisterDate DATETIME,
			VAL_FirstRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
			VAL_UserId INT,
			VAL_RegisterDate DATETIME,
			VAL_RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)
		)
	'
	


	/*Insert To Forms*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
		INSERT INTO @Forms_tbl(
			[FormId],
			[FormName],
			[FormTitle],
			[FormTitleColor],
			[FormBgColor],
			[FormHeader],
			[FormBody],
			[FormFooter],
			[HaveUploadFile],
			[UploadFileDescription],
			[UploadFileCount],
			[UploadFileMaxSize],
			[ColumnCount],
			[IsActive],
			[OrderView],
			[ParentFormId],
			[CrmSysPartId],
			[FOSysPartId],
			[FirstRegisterUserId],
			[FirstRegisterDate],
			[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
			[UserId],
			[RegisterDate],
			[RegisterDatePersian],
			[Year1],
			[Month1],
			[GUId],
			[AppType],
			[LanguageID],
			[UploadFileExtension],
			[PrintFormatId],
			[FOIsVisible],
			[FOIsEnable],
			[FOCanInsertMulti],
			[EnableForUsersId],
			[EnableForRollId],
			[CheckerUsersId],
			[WorkflowId],
			[CrmSysPartShow],
			[FOSysPartShow],
			[SetSysPartFieldByUser],
			[EnableForResponsibleUserChecker],
			[VerificationWorkFlowId]
		)
		SELECT  F.[FormId],
				F.[FormName],
				F.[FormTitle],
				F.[FormTitleColor],
				F.[FormBgColor],
				F.[FormHeader],
				F.[FormBody],
				F.[FormFooter],
				F.[HaveUploadFile],
				F.[UploadFileDescription],
				F.[UploadFileCount],
				F.[UploadFileMaxSize],
				F.[ColumnCount],
				F.[IsActive],
				F.[OrderView],
				F.[ParentFormId],
				F.[CrmSysPartId],
				F.[FOSysPartId],
				F.[FirstRegisterUserId],
				F.[FirstRegisterDate],
				F.[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
				F.[UserId],
				F.[RegisterDate],
				F.[RegisterDatePersian],
				F.[Year1],
				F.[Month1],
				F.[GUId],
				F.[AppType],
				F.[LanguageID],
				F.[UploadFileExtension],
				F.[PrintFormatId],
				F.[FOIsVisible],
				F.[FOIsEnable],
				F.[FOCanInsertMulti],
				F.[EnableForUsersId],
				F.[EnableForRollId],
				F.[CheckerUsersId],
				F.[WorkflowId],
				F.[CrmSysPartShow],
				F.[FOSysPartShow],
				F.[SetSysPartFieldByUser],
				F.[EnableForResponsibleUserChecker],
				F.[VerificationWorkFlowId]
			 FROM CrmDyForms F
			 LEFT JOIN [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] FFMV ON (F.[FormId] = FFMV.[FormId])
			 LEFT JOIN  [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues] FFV  ON (FFMV.[FormFieldMasterValueId] = FFV.[MasterValueId]) 
			WHERE (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM STRING_SPLIT(''' + @FormId + ''', '','') T WHERE ISNULL(T.[value], '''') <> '''' AND (CAST(T.[value] AS INT) = F.FormId OR CAST(T.[value] AS INT) = ParentFormId)) > 0
			  AND ISNULL(F.[IsActive],0) = 1 
			  AND (F.[FormId] IN (SELECT F.[FormId]      
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormUserAccess] FUA 
										WHERE FUA.[CrmUserId] = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ')  
								  OR F.[ParentFormId] IN (SELECT F.[FormId]      
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormUserAccess] FUA 
										WHERE FUA.[CrmUserId] = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' )) 
			  AND FFMV.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' UNION  
								SELECT LinkedUserId FROM [dbo].[CrmLinkedUsers] 
								WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 27 ) 
	'
	IF (@FirstRegisterUserId > '0')	
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND FFMV.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN ( ' + @FirstRegisterUserId  +') '	
	/**/
	IF @FromFirstRegisterDate IS NOT NULL AND @ToFirstRegisterDate IS NOT NULL	
	BEGIN
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +
		' AND FFMV.[FirstRegisterDate] BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FromFirstRegisterDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@ToFirstRegisterDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''			
	END
	/**/
	IF @FromRegisterDate IS NOT NULL AND @ToRegisterDate IS NOT NULL	
	BEGIN
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +
		' AND FFMV.[RegisterDate] BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FromRegisterDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@ToRegisterDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''			
	END
	/**/
	IF @CompanyId > '0'	
	BEGIN
		IF @CompanyId = '-20'
		BEGIN
			DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(max)
			SET @txt = 'CRMCompany'
			SELECT @txt = TXT
			FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
			WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
			AND TypeId = 1	
			/*--*/
			SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +
			' AND FFMV.[CompanyId] IN (  	
			SELECT CompanyId FROM ( ' + 
			@txt
			+ ' )t ) '	

			IF(ISNULL(@txt,'') <> '')
			BEGIN
			/**/
				DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
					ProjectId INT,
					CompanyPersonId INT
				)
				DECLARE @CmpRptQry VARCHAR(MAX) = '',@CmpRptPrjId INT = NULL,@CmpRptPrsId INT = NULL
				SET @CmpRptQry = ' 
				DECLARE @CmpRptTbl TABLE(
					ProjectId INT,
					CompanyPersonId INT
				)
	
				INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
				SELECT DISTINCT
					ProjectId,
					CompanyPersonId
				FROM ( ' +  @txt + ' )t 
				
				SELECT ProjectId,CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl'
						
				INSERT INTO @CmpRptTbl(ProjectId, CompanyPersonId)
				EXEC (@CmpRptQry)			
				/*--------------------------------*/
				SET @CmpRptPrjId = (SELECT TOP(1) ProjectId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(ProjectId,0) <> 0)
				SET @CmpRptPrsId = (SELECT TOP(1) CompanyPersonId FROM @CmpRptTbl WHERE ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) <> 0)
				/*--------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @CmpRptFrmSysPartId INT = NULL,@FrmHavePrj BIT = NULL,@FrmHavePrs BIT = NULL
				DECLARE @Parent_FormId INT 			
				SET @Parent_FormId = (SELECT TOP(1) FormId FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] WHERE FormId IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) 
																									FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@FormId,',') 
																									WHERE CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) <> 0)
																					AND ISNULL(ParentFormId,0) = 0)			
				SET @CmpRptFrmSysPartId = (SELECT TOP (1) [CrmSysPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] WHERE [FormId] = @Parent_FormId)


				SELECT @FrmHavePrj = 1
				WHERE @CmpRptFrmSysPartId IN(12,13,20,30,40,50,51,60,61)

				SELECT @FrmHavePrs = 1
				WHERE @CmpRptFrmSysPartId IN(11,13,20,30,4050,51,60,61)
				/*-----------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrjId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@FrmHavePrj,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +
					' AND  ISNULL(FFMV.[ProjectId],0) IN (  	
					SELECT ISNULL(ProjectId,0) FROM ( ' + 
					@txt
					+ ' )t ) '
				/**/
				END
				/*-----------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@CmpRptPrsId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@FrmHavePrs,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +
					' AND ISNULL(FFMV.[CompanyPersonId],0) IN (  	
					SELECT ISNULL(CompanyPersonId,0) FROM ( ' + 
					@txt
					+ ' )t) '

				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END		
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +
			' AND FFMV.[CompanyId] IN ( ' + @CompanyId +') '
		END		
	END
	/**/
	IF @RegisterUserId > '0'		
	BEGIN
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND FFMV.[UserId] IN ( ' + @RegisterUserId  +') '
	END 
	/**/
	IF @CheckedStatusId > '0'	
	BEGIN
		IF @CheckedStatusId = 1 
			SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND FFMV.[IsChecked] = 1 '
		
		ELSE
		IF @CheckedStatusId = 2 	
			SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND ISNULL(FFMV.[IsChecked], 0) = 0 '
			
	END
	/**/
	IF @MasterValueId IS NOT NULL AND @MasterValueId > 0	
	BEGIN
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND FFMV.[FormFieldMasterValueId] = ' + CAST(@MasterValueId AS NVARCHAR(100))		
	END
	/**/
	IF @FromCheckedDate IS NOT NULL AND @ToCheckedDate IS NOT NULL	
	BEGIN
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +
		' AND FFMV.[CheckedDate] BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FromCheckedDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@ToCheckedDate AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''			
	END
	/**/
	IF @CheckerUserId > '0'		
	BEGIN
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +
		' AND FFMV.[CheckerUserId] IN ( ' + @CheckerUserId  +') '	
	END 
	/**/
	IF @DyFormNumber > '0'	
	BEGIN
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND FFMV.[DyFormNumber] IN(' + @DyFormNumber + ') '		
	END
	/**/
	IF @VerificationWorkflowId > '0'
	BEGIN
	/**/	
		IF @CheckedStatusId > '0'		
		BEGIN
			IF @CheckedStatusId = 1 /*===> not expired*/
				SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +	' AND FFMV.[FormFieldMasterValueId] IN (					
				SELECT [ObjectId]      
				FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
				WHERE [ObjectType] = 80 /*DyForm*/
				AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowSetpId +' 
				AND [VerificationRequestId] IN (
					SELECT [VerificationRequestId]      
					FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
					WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId + ' 
					AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
					)
				) '
		
			ELSE IF @CheckedStatusId = 2 /*expired*/			
				SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +	'AND FFMV.[FormFieldMasterValueId] IN (
					SELECT [VerificationReqObjectId]      
					FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests]
					WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80 /*DyForm*/
					AND [VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId +'
					AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
					AND [VerificationRequestId] NOT IN (SELECT [VerificationRequestId]
															FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS LEFT JOIN
															(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRDI 
																WHERE  VRDI.[ObjectType] = 80 /*DyForm*/) VRD
																ON VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = VRD.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]
																WHERE VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId +'
																AND VWS.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowSetpId +')						
				 )'								
		END
		ELSE 
		BEGIN		
			SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry +	' AND FFMV.[FormFieldMasterValueId] IN (					
			SELECT [VerificationReqObjectId]      
			FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] VR LEFT JOIN
			(SELECT * FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRDI 
			WHERE  VRDI.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowSetpId +' AND VRDI.[ObjectType] = 80 /*DyForm*/) VRD
			ON VR.[VerificationRequestId] = VRD.[VerificationRequestId]
			WHERE [VerificationReqObjectType] = 80 /*DyForm*/
			AND VR.[VerificationWorkFlowId] = ' + @VerificationWorkflowId + '
			AND ISNULL([VerificationReqExpired],0) = 0
			) '		
		END	
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF @VerificationWorkflowId <= '0'
	BEGIN 
		IF @CheckedStatusId > '0'		
		BEGIN
			IF @CheckedStatusId =  1 
				SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND FFMV.[IsChecked] = 1 '
			ELSE 
			IF @CheckedStatusId = 2
				SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND ISNULL(FFMV.[IsChecked], 0) = 0 '		
		
		END		
	END
	/**/
	IF(@Values IS NOT NULL AND @Values <> '')
	BEGIN
		/*===================== Get Value =============================*/
		DECLARE @FieldVal NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL, @TmpTerm VARCHAR(MAX) = ''		
		WHILE LEN(@Values) > 0
		BEGIN
			IF PATINDEX('%|@Rpt^Frm@|%', @Values) > 0
			BEGIN
				SET @FieldVal = SUBSTRING(@Values, 0, PATINDEX('%|@Rpt^Frm@|%', @Values))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @FieldValueTo NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @FieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
				DECLARE @FieldType NVARCHAR(MAX)
				SET @FieldValue = NULL
				SET @FieldValueTo = NULL
				SET @FieldType = NULL
				/**/
				IF(CHARINDEX('@:@',@FieldVal) > 0)
					SET @FieldValue = SUBSTRING(@FieldVal, 1, CHARINDEX('@:@',@FieldVal)-1)   
				ELSE 
					SET @FieldValue = @FieldVal
				/**/	
				IF(CHARINDEX('@:@',@FieldVal) > 0)                                 
					SET @FieldValueTo = SUBSTRING(@FieldVal, CHARINDEX('@:@', @FieldVal)+3, CHARINDEX('@T:T@', @FieldVal) - (CHARINDEX('@:@',@FieldVal)+ 3))
				IF(CHARINDEX('@T:T@',@FieldVal) > 0) 
					SET @FieldType = SUBSTRING(@FieldVal, CHARINDEX('@T:T@', @FieldVal)+5, Len(@FieldVal))
				/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
				IF(@TmpTerm <> '')
				BEGIN
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND '
				END

				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' FFV.MasterValueId IN( ' 
							 + ' SELECT DISTINCT FFVCDN.MasterValueId '
							 + ' FROM '
							 + ' (SELECT 
										 DFVV1.[FormId]     				
										,[MasterValueId]'
				IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
				BEGIN
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ',DFVV1.[Value]'
				END
				ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
				BEGIN
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + '
						,(SELECT CAST(L.[Value] AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) FROM CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang L 
						  WHERE L.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' 
						    AND L.FieldValueId = FormFieldValueId 
							AND ISJSON(CAST(L.[Value] AS NVARCHAR(MAX))) = 1
							) AS [Value]
					'
				END
				/**/
				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + '
										,DFVV1.[FieldId]		
										,DFVV1.[FieldRepeatId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues]   DFV1 
									CROSS APPLY OPENJSON(ISNULL(DFV1.[Value],''[]''))
									WITH (
											[FormId] INT ''$.FormId'',
											[FieldId] INT ''$.FieldId'',
											[FieldRepeatId] BIGINT ''$.FieldRepeatId'',
											[Value] NVARCHAR(MAx) ''$.Value''
											)  
											AS DFVV1
									WHERE ISNULL(DFV1.[IsDeleted],0) = 0 									
									)FFVCDN '
							+ ' WHERE 1 = 1 '
						
				/**/
				IF(@FieldType IS NOT NULL AND @FieldType <> '' AND 
					@FieldValueTo IS NOT NULL AND @FieldValueTo <> '')
				BEGIN
					IF(@FieldType = '2')
					BEGIN
						SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm 
						+ ' AND (FormFieldValueId IN(SELECT FFV_I.FormFieldValueId FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues] FFV_I WHERE FFV_I.FormId IN(' + @FormId + '))'					
						+ 'AND (CASE WHEN ISNUMERIC(FFVCDN.[Value]) = 1 THEN  CONVERT(NUMERIC(18, 3), FFVCDN.[Value])  ELSE CAST(''0'' AS NUMERIC(18, 3)) END)  BETWEEN CAST(''' + @FieldValue  + ''' AS NUMERIC(18,3)) AND CAST(''' + @FieldValueTo + ''' AS NUMERIC(18,3)) )'
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm +
							' AND ((REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(FFVCDN.Value,''/'',''''),''.'',''''),'':'','''')) BETWEEN ''' + @FieldValue  + ''' AND ''' + @FieldValueTo + ''') '
					END
				END
				ELSE IF(@FieldType = '8')
				BEGIN
					SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@8', '')
					SET @FieldValue = (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE((@FieldValue) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm  + ' AND ' + '''' + 
							 @FieldValue + ''' IN (SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(						 
													(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE((FFVCDN.Value) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))						 						 
												,''|'')) '
				END	
				ELSE IF(@FieldType = '9' OR @FieldType = '10' OR @FieldType = '11')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@9', '')
					SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@10', '')
					SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@11', '')
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND (FFVCDN.Value = ''' + @FieldValue + ''') '
				/**/
				END	
				ELSE IF(@FieldType = '6' OR @FieldType = '7')
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@7', '')
					SET @FieldValue = REPLACE(@FieldValue, '@T:T@6', '')
					SET @FieldValue = (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE((@FieldValue) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
				

					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND ' +
						' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(FFVCDN.Value AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) =''' + @FieldValue + ''') '
				/**/
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' AND ' +
						' (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(FFVCDN.Value AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like ''%' +
							REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@FieldValue AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), NCHAR(1740),
							NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') '
				END														
				/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
				/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
				SET @Values = SUBSTRING(@Values,
										  LEN(@FieldVal + '|@Rpt^Frm@|') + 1,
										  LEN(@Values))

				SET @TmpTerm = @TmpTerm + ' )'

			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @FieldVal = @Values
				SET @Values = NULL        
			END
		END
		/*=============================================================*/
		IF(@TmpTerm <> '')
			SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry  + ' AND ( ' + @TmpTerm + ') '
		/*=============================================================*/		 
	END
	



	/*Set Permission*/
	DECLARE @CrmSysPart NVARCHAR(50), @FOSysPart NVARCHAR(50)
	DECLARE @CrmSysPartId INT, @FOSysPartId INT
	/**/
	SET @CrmSysPart = (SELECT  TOP 1 [Part] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] SP WHERE SP.[SystemPartId] IN
								(SELECT [CrmSysPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] F WHERE F.[FormId] IN(SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@FormId,','))))
	/**/
	SET @FOSysPart = (SELECT  TOP 1 [Part] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] SP WHERE SP.[SystemPartId] IN
								(SELECT [FOSysPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms] F WHERE F.[FormId] IN(SELECT * FROM dbo.fnSplitString(@FormId,','))))
	/**/
	EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @rptUserId
	/**/
	DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX)
	SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
	  FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
	 WHERE TypeId = 20
	   AND UserId = @rptUserId
	/**/
	IF(@cmpDataPermision IS NOT NULL AND @cmpDataPermision <> '')
	BEGIN
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND (FFMV.[CompanyId] IN (SELECT C.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany c WHERE 1=1 ' + @cmpDataPermision + ') OR FFMV.[CompanyId] IS NULL )' /*===> because in movemnet warehouse companyId is null*/	
	END
	/**/
	DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE TypeId = 10 AND UserId = @rptUserId
	/**/
	DECLARE @otherDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
	/**/
	IF((@CrmSysPart IS NOT NULL AND @CrmSysPart <> '') OR (@FOSysPart IS NOT NULL AND @FOSysPart <> ''))
	BEGIN
		IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Prs' OR @FOSysPart = 'Prs')
		BEGIN
			/*Prs*/
			IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		
			SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' ( [CompanyPersonId] IN (SELECT Prs.CompanyPersonID
									FROM dbo.[CrmCompanyPersons] Prs
									WHERE Prs.FirstRegisterUserId IN 
									(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23 ))) '

		END
		IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Prj' OR @FOSysPart = 'Prj')
		BEGIN
			/*Prj*/
			IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		
			SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' ( [ProjectId] IN (SELECT Prj.[ProjectId]
									FROM dbo.[CrmProjects] Prj
									WHERE Prj.prjStarterUserId IN 
									(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24))) '

		END

		IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Cnvs' OR @FOSysPart = 'Cnvs')
		BEGIN
			/*Cnvs*/
			IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		
			SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' ( [ConversationId] IN (SELECT Cnvs.[ConversationId]
									FROM dbo.[CrmConversation] Cnvs
									WHERE 
									Cnvs.FirstRegisterUserId IN 
									(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25)
									OR Cnvs.cnvsUserId IN 
									(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25)
									OR Cnvs.cnvsReferUserId IN 
									(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25)																
									)) '
		END
		/**/
		IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Pord' OR @FOSysPart = 'Pord')
		BEGIN
			/*Pord*/
			IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
		
			SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' ( [MasterObjectId] IN (SELECT Pord.PreOrderId
									FROM dbo.[WsPreOrder] Pord
									WHERE Pord.pordReferUserId IN 
									(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 15 ))) '

		END
		IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Ord' OR @FOSysPart = 'Ord')
		BEGIN
			/*Ord*/
			IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
			SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision + ' ( [MasterObjectId] IN (SELECT ord.OrderId
									FROM dbo.[WsOrder] ord
									WHERE ord.ordReferUserId IN 
									(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 16 ))) '
		END
		IF( @CrmSysPart = 'Bord' OR @FOSysPart = 'Bord')
		BEGIN
			/*Bord*/
			IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
			SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision + ' ( [MasterObjectId] IN (SELECT bord.BuyOrderId
									FROM dbo.[WSBuyOrder] bord
									WHERE bord.bordReferUserId IN (SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 17 ))) '

		END
		IF( @CrmSysPart = 'WhDoc' OR @FOSysPart = 'WhDoc' OR 
				 @CrmSysPart = 'WhDraft' OR @FOSysPart = 'WhDraft' OR 
				 @CrmSysPart = 'WhMovement' OR @FOSysPart = 'WhMovement')
		BEGIN
			/*Wrh*/
			IF(@otherDataPermision <> '') SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision +' OR '
			SET @otherDataPermision = @otherDataPermision + ' [MasterObjectId] IS NULL OR ( [MasterObjectId] IN (SELECT dc.DocId
									FROM dbo.[WSDoc] dc
									WHERE dc.FirstRegisterUserId IN (SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 18 ))) '
		END
		/**/	
		IF(@otherDataPermision <> '')
			SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' AND (  ' +  @otherDataPermision + ' ) '	
	END



	/*Insert To Form Fields*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
		INSERT INTO @FormFields_tbl (
			[FormFieldId],

		SELECT  [FormFieldId],

				[ParentFieldBgColor],

				[MultiItems],
				[DefaultValue],
				[IsActive],
				[ColumnPosition],
				[OrderView],
				[FirstRegisterUserId],
				[FirstRegisterDate],
				[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
				[UserId],
				[RegisterDate],
				[RegisterDatePersian],

				[Month1],
				[GUId],
				[AppType],
				[LanguageID],
				[RowPosition],
				[IsRepeatable],
				[FOIsRepeatable],
				[UploadFileMaxSize],
				[UploadFileExtension]
			FROM CrmDyFormFields FF 
			WHERE (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @Forms_tbl F WHERE F.FormID = FF.FormId OR F.FormID = FF.ParentFormId) > 0
			AND FieldTypeId NOT IN (1,3)
			AND (ISNULL(''' + ISNULL(@SelectedFieldsIds, '') + ''', '''') = '''' OR (ISNULL(''' + ISNULL(@SelectedFieldsIds, '') + ''', N'''') <> N''''
														AND FF.FormFieldId IN (SELECT CAST(T.[value] AS INT) 
																					FROM STRING_SPLIT(''' + ISNULL(@SelectedFieldsIds, '') + ''', N'','') T 
																				WHERE ISNULL(T.[value], N'''') <> N'''')))
	'
	


	/*Insert To Form Field Values*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
			INSERT INTO @FormFieldValues_tbl(
				VAL_FormId, 
				VAL_FieldId, 
				VAL_Value,
				VAL_CompanyId,
				VAL_CompanyPersonId,
				VAL_ProjectId,
				VAL_CnvsId,

				VAL_FirstRegisterDate,
				VAL_FirstRegisterDatePersian,
				VAL_UserId,
				VAL_RegisterDate,
				VAL_RegisterDatePersian)
			SELECT FFV.FormId, 
				   T.VAL_FieldId, 
				   T.VAL_Value,
				   FFV.CompanyId,
				   FFV.CompanyPersonId,
				   FFV.ProjectId,
				   FFV.ConversationId,
				   FFV.FirstRegisterUserId,
				   FFV.FirstRegisterDate,
				   FFV.FirstRegisterDatePersian,
				   FFV.UserId,
				   FFV.RegisterDate,
				   FFV.RegisterDatePersian
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues] FFV '
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + '
			 CROSS APPLY(SELECT VAL_FormId, 
								VAL_FieldId, '
	IF (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + ' VAL_Value '
	ELSE IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + '
			(SELECT VAL_Value_Lang FROM OPENJSON((SELECT TOP 1 L.Value FROM CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang L WHERE L.FieldValueId = FFV.FormFieldValueId AND L.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND ISJSON(CAST(L.[Value] AS NVARCHAR(MAX))) = 1))  
				WITH (
					VAL_FieldId_Lang INT N''$.FieldId''
					,VAL_Value_Lang NVARCHAR(MAX) N''$.Value''
				) FFVL
			   WHERE VAL_FieldId_Lang = VAL_FieldId
				) AS VAL_Value '
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + '
									 
							   FROM OPENJSON(FFV.[Value])
							   WITH (
										VAL_FormId INT N''$.FormId'', 
										VAL_FieldId INT N''$.FieldId'', 
										VAL_Value NVARCHAR(MAX) N''$.Value''
									)
							) T
				 WHERE FFV.FormId IN (SELECT F.FormID FROM @Forms_tbl F)
				   AND ISNULL(FFV.[IsDeleted],0) = 0
				   AND FFV.FormId IN (' + @FormId + ')
		'



	/*Create Chart JS Bars*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
		UPDATE @FormFields_tbl /*Create Chart JS Bars*/
			SET ChartItemsJs = 
				(
					CASE ' /*ChartItemsJs*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
						WHEN FieldTypeId IN (2) THEN/*Numeric*/
							(SELECT FFV.VAL_Value AS [Text],
									FFV.VAL_Value AS [Value],
									COUNT(*) AS CNT 
								FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
								WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
								  AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
								GROUP BY FFV.VAL_Value
								FOR JSON PATH)' /*Type 2 = Numeric*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
						WHEN FieldTypeId IN (4) THEN/*Date*/
							(SELECT FFV.VAL_Value AS [Text],
									FFV.VAL_Value AS [Value],
									COUNT(*) AS CNT 
								FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
								WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
								  AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
								GROUP BY FFV.VAL_Value
								FOR JSON PATH)' /*Type 4 = Date*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
						WHEN FieldTypeId IN (6) THEN/*ComboBox*/
							(
								SELECT (SELECT TOP 1 TMP.[value] FROM (
												SELECT E.[value],
														(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL))) AS ROWNUM
													FROM STRING_SPLIT(T.[value]/*Text1:Value1:10*/, N'':'') E
													WHERE ISNULL(E.[value], N'''') <> N''''
											) TMP
											WHERE TMP.ROWNUM = 1) AS [Text],
										(SELECT TOP 1 TMP.[value] FROM (
												SELECT E.[value],
														(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL))) AS ROWNUM
													FROM STRING_SPLIT(T.[value]/*Text1:Value1:10*/, N'':'') E
													WHERE ISNULL(E.[value], N'''') <> N''''
											) TMP
											WHERE TMP.ROWNUM = 2) AS [Value],
											(SELECT TOP 1 TMP.[value] FROM (
												SELECT E.[value],
														(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL))) AS ROWNUM
													FROM STRING_SPLIT(T.[value] /*Text1:Value1:10*/, N'':'') E
													WHERE ISNULL(E.[value], N'''') <> N''''
											) TMP
											WHERE TMP.ROWNUM = 3) AS [OrderView],
										(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
											WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
											AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
											AND VAL_Value = (SELECT TOP 1 TMP.[value] FROM (
																SELECT E.[value],
																		(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL))) AS ROWNUM
																	FROM STRING_SPLIT(T.[value]/*Text1:Value1:10*/, N'':'') E
																	WHERE ISNULL(E.[value], N'''') <> N''''
															) TMP
															WHERE TMP.ROWNUM = 2)
										)  AS [CNT]
									FROM STRING_SPLIT([MultiItems]/*Text1:Value1:10|Text2:Value2:20|Text3:Value3:30*/, N''|'') T
									FOR JSON PATH
							)' /*Type 6 = ComboBox*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
						WHEN FieldTypeId IN (7,8) THEN/*Radio,CheckBox*/
							(
								SELECT (SELECT TOP 1 TMP.[value] FROM (
												SELECT E.[value],
														(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL))) AS ROWNUM
													FROM STRING_SPLIT(T.[value], N'':'') E /*Text1:Value1:10*/
													WHERE ISNULL(E.[value], N'''') <> N''''
											) TMP
											WHERE TMP.ROWNUM = 1) AS [Text],
										(SELECT TOP 1 TMP.[value] FROM (
												SELECT E.[value],
														(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL))) AS ROWNUM
													FROM STRING_SPLIT(T.[value], N'':'') E /*Text1:Value1:10*/
													WHERE ISNULL(E.[value], N'''') <> N''''
											) TMP
											WHERE TMP.ROWNUM = 1) AS [Value],
											(SELECT TOP 1 TMP.[value] FROM (
												SELECT E.[value],
														(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL))) AS ROWNUM
													FROM STRING_SPLIT(T.[value], N'':'') E /*Text1:Value1:10*/
													WHERE ISNULL(E.[value], N'''') <> N''''
											) TMP
											WHERE TMP.ROWNUM = 2) AS [OrderView],
										(SELECT COUNT(*)
											FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV
											WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
											AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
																		SELECT E.[value],
																				(ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL))) AS ROWNUM
																			FROM STRING_SPLIT(T.[value], N'':'') E /*Text1:Value1:10*/
																			WHERE ISNULL(E.[value], N'''') <> N''''
																	) TMP
																	WHERE TMP.ROWNUM = 1
																	  AND TMP.[value] IN (
																							SELECT RVAL.[value]
																							  FROM STRING_SPLIT(FFV.VAL_Value/*Text1|Text2*/, N''|'') RVAL
																							 WHERE ISNULL(RVAL.[value], '''') <> ''''
																						 )
												) > 0
											) AS [CNT]
									FROM STRING_SPLIT([MultiItems], N''|'') T/*Text1:Value1:10|Text2:Value2:20|Text3:Value3:30*/
									FOR JSON PATH
							)' /*Type 7,8 = Radio,CheckBox*/
	IF (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)/*9,10,11*/
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
							WHEN FieldTypeId = 9 THEN
								(SELECT (SELECT C.cmpName FROM CrmCompany C WHERE C.CompanyID = CAST(ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT)) AS [Text],
										FFV.VAL_Value AS [Value],
										COUNT(*) AS [CNT]
									FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
									WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
									  AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
									GROUP BY FFV.VAL_Value
									FOR JSON PATH)' /*Type 9 = Company*/
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
							WHEN FieldTypeId = 10 THEN
								(SELECT (SELECT P.PrdName FROM WSProducts P WHERE P.ProductID = CAST(ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT)) AS [Text],
										FFV.VAL_Value AS [Value],
										COUNT(*) AS [CNT]
									FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
									WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
									  AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
									GROUP BY FFV.VAL_Value
									FOR JSON PATH)' /*Type 10 = Product*/
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
							WHEN FieldTypeId = 11 THEN
								(SELECT (SELECT ISNULL(U.FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.LName, '''') FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = CAST(ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT)) AS [Text],
										FFV.VAL_Value AS [Value],
										COUNT(*) AS [CNT]
									FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
									WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
									  AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
									GROUP BY FFV.VAL_Value
									FOR JSON PATH)' /*Type 11 = User*/
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
							WHEN FieldTypeId = 9 THEN
								(SELECT (SELECT C.cmpName FROM CrmCompany_Lang C WHERE C.CompanyID = CAST(ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT) AND C.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') AS [Text],
										FFV.VAL_Value AS [Value],
										COUNT(*) AS [CNT]
									FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
									WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
									  AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
									GROUP BY FFV.VAL_Value
									FOR JSON PATH)' /*Type 9 = Company*/
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
							WHEN FieldTypeId = 10 THEN
								(SELECT (SELECT P.PrdName FROM WSProducts P WHERE P.ProductID = CAST(ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT) AND P.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') AS [Text],
										FFV.VAL_Value AS [Value],
										COUNT(*) AS [CNT]
									FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
									WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
									  AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
									GROUP BY FFV.VAL_Value
									FOR JSON PATH)' /*Type 10 = Product*/
		SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
							WHEN FieldTypeId = 11 THEN
								(SELECT (SELECT ISNULL(U.FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.LName, '''') FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = CAST(ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT) AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') AS [Text],
										FFV.VAL_Value AS [Value],
										COUNT(*) AS [CNT]
									FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV 
									WHERE FFV.VAL_FieldId = FormFieldId
									  AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
									GROUP BY FFV.VAL_Value
									FOR JSON PATH)' /*Type 11 = User*/
		/**/
	END
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
						ELSE 
							NULL
					END
				)
	' /*Else*/
	/*!Create Chart JS*/



	SET @Term = @Term + ' FROM @FormFields_tbl FF
						 WHERE 1=1'
						 

	/*====================== Set Qry ========================*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = ' SELECT DISTINCT
			FF.*,
			(
				SELECT FFV.VAL_CompanyId AS CompanyId
					,(
						CASE 
							WHEN ISNULL((
										SELECT C.cmpKind
										FROM CrmCompany C
										WHERE C.CompanyID = FFV.VAL_CompanyId
										), 0) = 0
								THEN (
										(
												SELECT CompanyTitle
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT
												WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = (
														SELECT C.cmpTitleId
														FROM CrmCompany C
														WHERE C.CompanyID = FFV.VAL_CompanyId
														)
												) + '' '' + (
												SELECT C.cmpName
												FROM CrmCompany C
												WHERE C.CompanyID = FFV.VAL_CompanyId
												)
										)
							ELSE (
									SELECT ISNULL((
												SELECT PersonTitle
												FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT
												WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId
												), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName, '''')
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
									WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = (
											SELECT C.cmpMainPersonId
											FROM CrmCompany C
											WHERE C.CompanyID = FFV.VAL_CompanyId
											)
									)
							END
						) CmpNameWithTitle
					,FFV.VAL_CompanyPersonId AS CompanyPersonId
					,(
						SELECT ISNULL((
									SELECT CT.PersonTitle
									FROM CrmPersonTitle CT
									WHERE CT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId
									), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CP.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CP.prsLName, '''')
						FROM CrmCompanyPersons CP
						WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = FFV.VAL_CompanyPersonId
						) AS PrsName
					,VAL_ProjectId AS ProjectId
					,VAL_CnvsId AS ConversationId
					,VAL_FirstRegisterUserId AS FirstRegisterUserId
					,(SELECT ISNULL(U.FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.LName, '''') FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = FFV.VAL_FirstRegisterUserId) AS FirstRegisterUser
					,VAL_FirstRegisterDate AS FirstRegisterDate
					,VAL_FirstRegisterDatePersian AS FirstRegisterDatePersian
					,VAL_UserId AS UserId
					,(SELECT ISNULL(U.FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.LName, '''') FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = FFV.VAL_UserId) AS EditUser
					,VAL_RegisterDate AS RegisterDate
					,VAL_RegisterDatePersian AS RegisterDatePersian
					,(
						CASE 
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 6 THEN (
									SELECT LSTTMP.[value] FROM (
										SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL)) RW,
												TMP.[value]
											FROM STRING_SPLIT(REPLACE(MultiItems,'':'', N''|''), N''|'') TMP
									) AS LSTTMP
									WHERE RW = (
										SELECT TOP 1 (RW-1) AS RW FROM (
											SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL)) RW,
												T.[value]
											FROM string_split(REPLACE(MultiItems,'':'', N''|''), N''|'') T
										) LST
										WHERE [value] = VAL_Value
									)
								)
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 8 THEN (
									REPLACE(LEFT(VAL_Value, LEN(VAL_Value)-1), N''|'', N'', '')
								)
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 9 THEN
							(
								CASE 
									WHEN ISNULL((
												SELECT C.cmpKind
												FROM CrmCompany C
												WHERE C.CompanyID = VAL_Value
												), 0) = 0
										THEN (
												(
													SELECT CompanyTitle
													FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT
													WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = (
															SELECT C.cmpTitleId
															FROM CrmCompany C
															WHERE C.CompanyID = VAL_Value
															)
													) + '' '' + (
													SELECT C.cmpName
													FROM CrmCompany C
													WHERE C.CompanyID = VAL_Value
													)
												)
									ELSE (
											SELECT ISNULL((
														SELECT PersonTitle
														FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT
														WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId
														), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName, '''')
											FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
											WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = (
													SELECT C.cmpMainPersonId
													FROM CrmCompany C
													WHERE C.CompanyID = VAL_Value
													)
											)
								END
							)
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 10 THEN 
								(SELECT P.PrdName FROM WSProducts P WHERE P.ProductID = CAST(ISNULL(VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT))
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 11 THEN 
								(SELECT ISNULL(FName + '' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM Users U WHERE U.UserId = CAST(ISNULL(VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT))
							ELSE 
								FFV.VAL_Value
						END
					) AS [ItemValue]
				FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV
			   WHERE FFV.VAL_FormId = FF.FormId
			     AND FFV.VAL_FieldId = FF.FormFieldId
				 AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
				 FOR JSON PATH
			) AS DetailsInfo /*This Field Show Bars Data*/
		'
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		SET @Qry = ' SELECT
			FF.*,
			(
				SELECT 
					FFV.VAL_CompanyId AS CompanyId
					,(
						CASE 
							WHEN ISNULL((
										SELECT C.cmpKind
										FROM CrmCompany C
										WHERE C.CompanyID = FFV.VAL_CompanyId
										), 0) = 0
								THEN (
										(
												SELECT (
														SELECT CompanyTitle
														FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CTL
														WHERE CTL.CompanyTitleID = CT.CompanyTitleID
															AND CTL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
														)
												FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT
												WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = (
														SELECT C.cmpTitleId
														FROM CrmCompany C
														WHERE C.CompanyID = FFV.VAL_CompanyId
														)
												) + '' '' + (
												SELECT (
														SELECT CL.cmpName
														FROM CrmCompany_Lang CL
														WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID
															AND CL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
														)
												FROM CrmCompany C
												WHERE C.CompanyID = FFV.VAL_CompanyId
												)
										)
							ELSE (
									SELECT ISNULL((
												SELECT (
														SELECT PTL.PersonTitle
														FROM CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTL
														WHERE PTL.PersonTitleID = PT.PersonTitleID
															AND PTL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
														)
												FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT
												WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId
												), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL((
												SELECT PL.prsFName
												FROM CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PL
												WHERE PL.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID
													AND PL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
												), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL((
												SELECT PL.prsLName
												FROM CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PL
												WHERE PL.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID
													AND PL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
												), '''')
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
									WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = (
											SELECT C.cmpMainPersonId
											FROM CrmCompany C
											WHERE C.CompanyID = FFV.VAL_CompanyId
											)
									)
							END
						) CmpNameWithTitle
					,FFV.VAL_CompanyPersonId AS CompanyPersonId
					,(
						SELECT ISNULL((
									SELECT CT.PersonTitle
									FROM CrmPersonTitle_Lang CT
									WHERE CT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId
										AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
									), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL((
									SELECT CPL.prsFName
									FROM CrmCompanyPersons CPL
									WHERE CPL.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID
										AND CPL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
									), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL((
									SELECT CPL.prsLName
									FROM CrmCompanyPersons CPL
									WHERE CPL.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID
										AND CPL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
									), '''')
						FROM CrmCompanyPersons CP
						WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = FFV.VAL_CompanyPersonId
						) AS PrsName
					,VAL_ProjectId AS ProjectId
					,VAL_CnvsId AS ConversationId
					,VAL_FirstRegisterUserId AS FirstRegisterUserId
					,(SELECT ISNULL(U.FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.LName, '''') FROM Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = FFV.VAL_FirstRegisterUserId AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') AS FirstRegisterUser
					,VAL_FirstRegisterDate AS FirstRegisterDate
					,VAL_FirstRegisterDatePersian AS FirstRegisterDatePersian
					,VAL_UserId AS UserId
					,(SELECT ISNULL(U.FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.LName, '''') FROM Users_Lang  U WHERE U.UserId = FFV.VAL_UserId AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ') AS EditUser
					,VAL_RegisterDate AS RegisterDate
					,VAL_RegisterDatePersian AS RegisterDatePersian
					,(
						CASE 
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 6 THEN (
									SELECT LSTTMP.[value] FROM (
										SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL)) RW,
												TMP.[value]
											FROM STRING_SPLIT(REPLACE((SELECT FFL.MultiItems FROM CrmDyFormFields_Lang FFL WHERE FFL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND FFL.FieldId = FF.FormFieldId),'':'', N''|''), N''|'') TMP
									) AS LSTTMP
									WHERE RW = (
										SELECT TOP 1 (RW-1) AS RW FROM (
											SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT NULL)) RW,
												T.[value]
											FROM string_split(REPLACE((SELECT FFL.MultiItems FROM CrmDyFormFields_Lang FFL WHERE FFL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ' AND FFL.FieldId = FF.FormFieldId),'':'', N''|''), N''|'') T
										) LST
										WHERE [value] = VAL_Value
									)
								)
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 8 THEN (
									REPLACE(LEFT(VAL_Value, LEN(VAL_Value)-1), N''|'', N'', '')
								)
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 9 THEN 
							(
								CASE 
									WHEN ISNULL((
												SELECT C.cmpKind
												FROM CrmCompany C
												WHERE C.CompanyID = VAL_Value
												), 0) = 0
										THEN (
												(
													SELECT (
															SELECT CompanyTitle
															FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CTL
															WHERE CTL.CompanyTitleID = CT.CompanyTitleID
																AND CTL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
															)
													FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT
													WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = (
															SELECT C.cmpTitleId
															FROM CrmCompany C
															WHERE C.CompanyID = VAL_Value
															)
													) + '' '' + (
													SELECT (
															SELECT CL.cmpName
															FROM CrmCompany_Lang CL
															WHERE CL.CompanyID = C.CompanyID
																AND CL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
															)
													FROM CrmCompany C
													WHERE C.CompanyID = VAL_Value
													)
												)
									ELSE (
											SELECT ISNULL((
														SELECT (
																SELECT PTL.PersonTitle
																FROM CrmPersonTitle_Lang PTL
																WHERE PTL.PersonTitleID = PT.PersonTitleID
																	AND PTL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
																)
														FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT
														WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId
														), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL((
														SELECT PL.prsFName
														FROM CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PL
														WHERE PL.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID
															AND PL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
														), '''') + '' '' + ISNULL((
														SELECT PL.prsLName
														FROM CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PL
														WHERE PL.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID
															AND PL.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + '
														), '''')
											FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
											WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = (
													SELECT C.cmpMainPersonId
													FROM CrmCompany C
													WHERE C.CompanyID = VAL_Value
													)
											)
								END
							)
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 10 THEN 
								(SELECT P.PrdName FROM WSProducts_Lang P WHERE P.ProductID = CAST(ISNULL(VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT) AND P.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ')
							WHEN FF.FieldTypeId = 11 THEN 
								(SELECT ISNULL(FName + '' '', '''') + ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = CAST(ISNULL(VAL_Value, ''0'') AS INT) AND U.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(50)) + ')
							ELSE 
								FFV.VAL_Value
						END
					) AS [ItemValue]
				FROM @FormFieldValues_tbl FFV
			   WHERE FFV.VAL_FormId = FF.FormId
			     AND FFV.VAL_FieldId = FF.FormFieldId
				 AND ISNULL(FFV.VAL_Value, N'''') <> N''''
				 FOR JSON PATH
			) AS DetailsInfo /*This Field Show Bars Data*/
		'
	END
	/*=================== Execute Query ==================== */
	
	EXECUTE(@BeforeQry + ' ' + @Qry + @Term + ' AND FF.[ParentFormId] = 0 ORDER BY [FirstRegisterDate]')
	/*SELECT(@BeforeQry + ' ' + @Qry + @Term + ' AND FF.[ParentFormId] = 0 ORDER BY [FirstRegisterDate]')*/
	/**/
END







GO

/*NKR14021124*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CRMspRpt_ChartDyFormDateFieldType]
(
	/*
		exec [dbo].[CRMspRpt_ChartDyFormDateFieldType] @FormId=N'176,177,178,179',@FieldId=N'454,5097,504',@Values=N'0@:@0@T:T@2|@Rpt^Frm@|سفید@T:T@7|@Rpt^Frm@|سیکل@T:T@6|@Rpt^Frm@|',
		@rptUserId=3,@FromFirstRegisterDate=NULL,@ToFirstRegisterDate=NULL,@FromRegisterDate=NULL,@ToRegisterDate=NULL,@CompanyId=N'0',
		@FirstRegisterUserId=N'0',@RegisterUserId=N'0',@CheckedStatusId=N'0',@LanguageID=1065,@DefaultLanguageID=1065,@MasterValueId=0,@FromCheckedDate=NULL,
		@ToCheckedDate=NULL,@CheckerUserId=N'0',@DyFormNumber=N'0',@VerificationWorkflowId=N'0',@VerificationWorkflowSetpId=N'0'


		exec [dbo].[CRMspRpt_ChartDyFormDateFieldType] @FormId=N'176,177,178,179',@FieldId=N'454',@Values=N'0@:@0@T:T@2|@Rpt^Frm@|',@rptUserId=3,@FromFirstRegisterDate=NULL,@ToFirstRegisterDate=NULL,
		@FromRegisterDate=NULL,@ToRegisterDate=NULL,@CompanyId=N'0',@FirstRegisterUserId=N'0',@RegisterUserId=N'0',@CheckedStatusId=N'0',@LanguageID=1065,@DefaultLanguageID=1065,@MasterValueId=0,
		@FromCheckedDate=NULL,@ToCheckedDate=NULL,@CheckerUserId=N'0',@DyFormNumber=N'0',@VerificationWorkflowId=N'0',@VerificationWorkflowSetpId=N'0',@SelectedFieldsIds=N''

	*/
	@FormId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@FieldId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@Values NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@rptUserId INT,
	@FromFirstRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@ToFirstRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@FromRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@ToRegisterDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@CompanyId NVARCHAR(4000),
	@FirstRegisterUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@RegisterUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@CheckedStatusId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
	@MasterValueId INT = NULL,
	@FromCheckedDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@ToCheckedDate DateTime = NULL, 
	@CheckerUserId NVARCHAR(2000),
	@DyFormNumber NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@VerificationWorkflowId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@VerificationWorkflowSetpId NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
	@SelectedFieldsIds NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/

	DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = '', 
			@Term VARCHAR(MAX) = '',
			@BeforeQry NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''


	/*Create Form Temp Table*/
	SET @BeforeQry = N'
		DECLARE @Forms_tbl TABLE(
			[FormId] [int] NULL,
			[FormName] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[FormTitle] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[FormTitleColor] [nvarchar](50) NULL,
			[FormBgColor] [nvarchar](50) NULL,
			[FormHeader] [ntext] NULL,
			[FormBody] [ntext] NULL,
			[FormFooter] [ntext] NULL,
			[HaveUploadFile] [bit] NULL,
			[UploadFileDescription] [nvarchar](4000) NULL,
			[UploadFileCount] [int] NULL,
			[UploadFileMaxSize] [int] NULL,
			[ColumnCount] [int] NULL,
			[IsActive] [bit] NULL,
			[OrderView] [int] NULL,
			[ParentFormId] [int] NULL,
			[CrmSysPartId] [int] NULL,
			[FOSysPartId] [int] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterUserId] [int] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterDate] [datetime] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterDatePersian] [varchar](8) NULL,
			[UserId] [int] NULL,
			[RegisterDate] [datetime] NULL,
			[RegisterDatePersian] [nvarchar](8) NULL,
			[Year1] [smallint] NULL,
			[Month1] [smallint] NULL,
			[GUId] [uniqueidentifier] NULL,
			[AppType] [varchar](20) NULL,
			[LanguageID] [int] NULL,
			[UploadFileExtension] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[PrintFormatId] [int] NULL,
			[FOIsVisible] [bit] NULL,
			[FOIsEnable] [bit] NULL,
			[FOCanInsertMulti] [bit] NULL,
			[EnableForUsersId] [nvarchar](4000) NULL,
			[EnableForRollId] [int] NULL,
			[CheckerUsersId] [nvarchar](4000) NULL,
			[WorkflowId] [int] NULL,
			[CrmSysPartShow] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[FOSysPartShow] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[SetSysPartFieldByUser] [bit] NULL,
			[EnableForResponsibleUserChecker] [bit] NULL,
			[VerificationWorkFlowId] [int] NULL
		)
		'



	/*Create Form Field Temp Table*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
		DECLARE @FormFields_tbl TABLE (
			[FormFieldId] [int] NULL,
			[ParentFormId] [int] NULL,
			[FormId] [int] NULL,
			[FieldTypeId] [int] NULL,
			[ParentFieldId] [int] NULL,
			[ParentFieldColor] [nvarchar](50) NULL,
			[ParentFieldBgColor] [nvarchar](50) NULL,
			[FieldTitle] [nvarchar](max) NULL,
			[IsRequired] [bit] NULL,
			[RegularExpressionId] [int] NULL,
			[RegularExpressionErrorTxt] [nvarchar](1000) NULL,
			[MinValue] [bigint] NULL,
			[MaxValue] [bigint] NULL,
			[StrLength] [bigint] NULL,
			[PlaceholderTxt] [nvarchar](4000) NULL,
			[MultiItems] [nvarchar](max) NULL,
			[ChartItemsJs] [nvarchar](max) NULL,
			[DefaultValue] [nvarchar](max) NULL,
			[IsActive] [bit] NULL,
			[ColumnPosition] [int] NULL,
			[OrderView] [int] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterUserId] [int] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterDate] [datetime] NULL,
			[FirstRegisterDatePersian] [varchar](8) NULL,
			[UserId] [int] NULL,
			[RegisterDate] [datetime] NULL,
			[RegisterDatePersian] [nvarchar](8) NULL,
			[Year1] [smallint] NULL,
			[Month1] [smallint] NULL,
			[GUId] [uniqueidentifier] NULL,
			[AppType] [varchar](20) NULL,
			[LanguageID] [int] NULL,
			[RowPosition] [int] NULL,
			[IsRepeatable] [bit] NULL,
			[FOIsRepeatable] [bit] NULL,
			[UploadFileMaxSize] [int] NULL,
			[UploadFileExtension] [nvarchar](1000) NULL
		)
		'



	/*Create Field Values Temp Table*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
		DECLARE @FormFieldValues_tbl TABLE (
			VAL_FormId INT, 
			VAL_FieldId INT, 
			VAL_Value NVARCHAR(MAX),
			VAL_CompanyId INT,
			VAL_CompanyPersonId INT,
			VAL_ProjectId INT,
			VAL_CnvsId INT,
			VAL_FirstRegisterUserId INT,
			VAL_FirstRegisterDate DATETIME,
			VAL_FirstRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
			VAL_UserId INT,
			VAL_RegisterDate DATETIME,
			VAL_RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)
		)
	'
	


	/*Insert To Forms*/
	SET @BeforeQry = @BeforeQry + N'
		INSERT INTO @Forms_tbl(
			[FormId],
			[FormName],
			[FormTitle],
			[FormTitleColor],
			[FormBgColor],
			[FormHeader],
			[FormBody],
			[FormFooter],
			[HaveUploadFile],
			[UploadFileDescription],
			[UploadFileCount],
			[UploadFileMaxSize],
			[ColumnCount],
			[IsActive],
			[OrderView],
			[ParentFormId],
			[CrmSysPartId],
			[FOSysPartId],
			[FirstRegisterUserId],
			[FirstRegisterDate],
			[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
			[UserId],
			[RegisterDate],
			[RegisterDatePersian],
			[Year1],
			[Month1],
			[GUId],
			[AppType],
			[LanguageID],
			[UploadFileExtension],
			[PrintFormatId],
			[FOIsVisible],
			[FOIsEnable],
			[FOCanInsertMulti],
			[EnableForUsersId],
			[EnableForRollId],
			[CheckerUsersId],
			[WorkflowId],
			[CrmSysPartShow],
			[FOSysPartShow],
			[SetSysPartFieldByUser],
			[EnableForResponsibleUserChecker],
			[VerificationWorkFlowId]
		)
		SELECT  F.[FormId],
				F.[FormName],
				F.[FormTitle],
				F.[FormTitleColor],
				F.[FormBgColor],
				F.[FormHeader],
				F.[FormBody],
				F.[FormFooter],
				F.[HaveUploadFile],
				F.[UploadFileDescription],
				F.[UploadFileCount],
				F.[UploadFileMaxSize],
				F.[ColumnCount],
				F.[IsActive],
				F.[OrderView],
				F.[ParentFormId],
				F.[CrmSysPartId],
				F.[FOSysPartId],
				F.[FirstRegisterUserId],
				F.[FirstRegisterDate],
				F.[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
				F.[UserId],
				F.[RegisterDate],
				F.[RegisterDatePersian],
				F.[Year1],
				F.[Month1],
				F.[GUId],
				F.[AppType],
				F.[LanguageID],
				F.[UploadFileExtension],
				F.[PrintFormatId],
				F.[FOIsVisible],
				F.[FOIsEnable],
				F.[FOCanInsertMulti],
				F.[EnableForUsersId],
				F.[EnableForRollId],
				F.[CheckerUsersId],
				F.[WorkflowId],
				F.[CrmSysPartShow],
				F.[FOSysPartShow],
				F.[SetSysPartFieldByUser],
				F.[EnableForResponsibleUserChecker],
				F.[VerificationWorkFlowId]
			 FROM CrmDyForms F
			 LEFT JOIN [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] FFMV ON (F.[FormId] = FFMV.[FormId])
			 LEFT JOIN  [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues] FFV  ON (FFMV.[FormFieldMasterValueId] = FFV.[MasterValueId]) 
			WHERE (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM STRING_SPLIT(''' + @FormId + ''', '','') T WHERE ISNULL(T.[value], '''') <> '''' AND (CAST(T.[value] AS INT) = F.FormId OR CAST(T.[value] AS INT) = ParentFormId)) > 0
			  AND ISNULL(F.[IsActive],0) = 1 
			  AND (F.[FormId] IN (SELECT F.[FormId]      
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormUserAccess] FUA 
										WHERE FUA.[CrmUserId] = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ')  
								  OR F.[ParentFormId] IN (SELECT F.[FormId]      
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormUserAccess] FUA 
										WHERE FUA.[CrmUserId] = ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS NVARCHAR(10)) + ' )) 
			  AND FFMV.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
								(SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(MAX)) + '
